### [1] [LitCurate: A Configuration-Driven AI-Assisted Framework for Scientific Database Construction with an Application to Lower-Mantle Equation-of-State Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27629)
*Abin Shakya,Wilson Samuels,Dominica Wilson,Gioia A. Marchi,Israa Draz,Chenxing Luo,Renata M. Wentzcovitch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LitCurate，一个基于大语言模型的开源框架，用于从科学文献中构建结构化数据库。它采用可审计的分阶段策展工作流，整合文献发现、相关性筛选、全文处理和结构化信息提取，并在下地幔高压矿物相状态方程数据库构建中验证了其有效性（1,334条记录来自205篇论文）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量科学文献中的实验和计算结果虽可用于数据驱动和物理建模，但这些信息仍被锁定在出版物中，难以被大规模分析或科学软件直接使用。从文献构建结构化数据库的主要挑战在于：需要从海量论文中发现相关研究，并且提取的数量必须附带足够的科学上下文才能保持可用性。

Method: LitCurate采用一个可审计的、分阶段的策展工作流，包括四个核心阶段：文献发现、相关性筛选、全文处理和结构化信息提取。该框架保留中间结果和来源信息（provenance），使研究者能够检查和修订各个阶段，而非将自动化策展视为黑盒过程。它利用大语言模型进行信息抽取，并区分source-reported和citation-reported的值。

Result: 将LitCurate应用于构建下地幔及下地幔相关高压矿物相的状态方程数据库，从实验和理论研究文献中提取了205篇论文的1,334条条目。生成的数据库将状态方程参数与矿物相、化学成分、方程形式、方法和参数约束相关联，并在可确定来源时标记值为source-reported或citation-reported。记录可通过可搜索的Web应用获取。

Conclusion: LitCurate通过将科学文献与可追溯的、机器可读的数据连接起来，为将累积的文献资源转化为可用于科学分析和计算建模的数据提供了可复用的方法，弥合了文献知识与大规模数据分析之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The growing scientific literature contains decades of experimental and computational results that could support data-driven and physics-based modeling, yet much of this infor- mation remains locked in publications and is not readily usable for large-scale analysis or sci- entific software. Building structured databases from the literature is particularly challenging whenrelevantstudiesmustfirstbediscoveredamonglargecollectionsofpapersandreported quantities must be extracted with enough scientific context to remain usable. We present LitCurate, an open-source framework for building scientific databases from the literature using large language models within an auditable, stage-wise curation workflow. LitCurate integratesliteraturediscovery, relevancescreening, full-textprocessing, andstructuredinfor- mation extraction while retaining intermediate results and provenance, allowing researchers to inspect and revise individual stages rather than treating automated curation as a black- box process. We apply LitCurate to construct an equation-of-state database of lower-mantle and lower-mantle-relevant high-pressure mineral phases from experimental and theoretical studies, comprising 1,334 entries from 205 papers. The resulting dataset links reported equation-of-state parameters to mineral phases, compositions, equation formulations, meth- ods, and parameter constraints, and labels values as source-reported or citation-reported when provenance can be determined. The records are available through a searchable web application. By connecting scientific literature to traceable, machine-readable data, LitCu- rate provides a reusable approach for transforming accumulated literature into resources for scientific analysis and computational modeling.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond the Vacuum: Combinatorial Strategy Selection for Competitor-Aware Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27631)
*Vaibhav Sourirajan,Yao Zhang,Himanshu Kumar,Sahil Wadhwa,Mann Patel,Amirfarrokh Iranitalab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑竞争对手感知的GEO（生成式引擎优化）策略选择方法。传统GEO方法孤立地选择重写策略，忽略了内容优化采用率增长时最优策略会随之变化这一外部性问题。作者将GEO形式化为一个竞争对手感知的策略选择问题，提出两阶段流水线：先用BOCS高效搜索重写策略空间，再从黑盒观测中生成偏好对和推理轨迹来微调语言模型，使其能分析文档语料并提出最优重写策略组合。在geo-bench和新增的竞争数据集geo-bench_comp上取得了SOTA性能，并能迁移到多个OOD数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GEO方法在选择重写策略时是孤立进行的，忽略了关键的负外部性：随着越来越多内容采用优化策略，原本最优的策略会因竞争环境的变化而失效。这一问题尚未被系统性地建模和解决。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 使用BOCS（组合结构的贝叶斯优化）高效搜索重写策略组合空间；(2) 从BOCS黑盒观测中生成偏好对和基于证据的推理轨迹，用于微调语言模型，使其能直接分析文档语料并输出最优的重写策略组合。

Result: 在geo-bench和合成的竞争性数据集geo-bench_comp上，在多个印象指标上超越了现有的智能体方法和单一启发式方法，达到SOTA水平。方法还能迁移到多个分布外(OOD)数据集，在不同领域、查询和文档类型上均表现有效。

Conclusion: 竞争对手感知的策略选择对GEO至关重要，结合贝叶斯优化与语言模型微调的两阶段方法能够有效应对动态竞争环境下的内容优化问题，且具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) has emerged as a novel paradigm for transforming content to increase visibility in Large Language Model (LLM) responses. Traditional GEO methods, however, select rewriting strategies in isolation, ignoring a critical externality: as adoption of content optimization grows, optimal strategies for rewriting content change. We formalize GEO as a competitor-aware strategy selection problem and propose a two-phase pipeline to solve it: (1) We use Bayesian Optimization of Combinatorial Structures (BOCS) to efficiently search the space of rewriting strategies, (2) We generate preference pairs and grounded reasoning traces from the BOCS black-box observations to fine-tune a language model to analyze a document corpus and propose optimal rewriting strategy combinations. We achieve state-of-the-art performance across several impression metrics over existing agentic and single-heuristic methods on both geo-bench and our synthetically augmented competitive dataset geo-bench_comp. Our method also transfers to multiple out-of-distribution datasets, proving effective across domains, queries, and document types.

</details>


### [3] [A Versioned Unified Graph Index for Dynamic Timestamp-Aware Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27663)
*Jun Woo Chung,Weijie Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TiGER（时间集成图用于高效检索），一种在动态向量数据集上执行快速时间感知近似最近邻搜索的方法，通过构建和维护统一图结构（基于集成版本化连通性），支持任意时间范围的直接查询，无需后过滤或合并，QPS提升最高达5倍且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态向量数据集上的时间感知近似最近邻搜索方法存在效率瓶颈：要么依赖搜索后过滤，要么需要为每个可能的时间段构建独立子图，导致查询开销大且难以灵活应对任意时间范围。

Method: 构建并维护一个统一的时间集成图，利用集成版本化连通性（integrated versioned connectivity）作为索引结构，使得任意时间间隔的查询可以直接在统一图上执行，而无需遍历无效向量。

Result: 与基于过滤或按时间段划分的子图基线方法相比，在保持精度的前提下，每秒查询数（QPS）提升最高达5倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有望在实时推荐系统、日志分析及任何需要对动态、时间分段数据进行快速相似性搜索的场景中实现高效的时间分析。

Abstract: We present TiGER (Time-Integrated Graph for Efficient Retrieval), a novel approach for performing fast time-aware approximate nearest neighbor searches on dynamic vector datasets with flexibility over any possible time range. Our proposed algorithm builds and maintains a unified graph for all vectors by leveraging an index structure based on integrated versioned connectivity, allowing arbitrary time intervals to be queried directly on the unified graph without having to traverse invalid vectors. This forgoes the need for post-search filtering or merging, or separate graphs for each possible composite range. Empirical evaluations show that our method attains up to a 5x improvement in queries per second (QPS) without compromising accuracy over baselines based on filtering or per-time-segment sub-graphs. We believe that this method will enable efficient temporal analysis across evolving datasets in real-time recommendation systems, log analysis, and any scenario requiring fast similarity search over dynamic, time-segmented data.

</details>


### [4] [NormasTCU --- A Brazilian Portuguese IR Dataset and an Evaluation of LLM-as-a-Judge for Relevance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27746)
*Leandro Carísio Fernandes,Marcus Vinícius Borela de Castro,Leandro dos Santos Ribeiro,Leonardo Augusto da Silva Pacheco,Edans Flávius de Oliveira Sandes*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NormasTCU——巴西葡萄牙语法律领域的IR数据集（14,469篇法律文档、46个查询、3,048条人工判断），并系统评估了LLM作为相关性判断器（LLM-as-a-judge）在该专业非英语领域的可靠性。研究发现LLM判断存在正向评分偏差，与人类判断的一致性仅达到中等水平，但在nDCG@10和MRR等排序感知指标下能产生高度相似的系统排名，表明LLM可支持大规模相关性评估，但应避免用于依赖精确率或召回率的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄牙语信息检索领域缺乏公开数据集，专业领域的相关性评估成本高昂。尽管LLM越来越多地被用于相关性评估，但其在非英语专业领域的可靠性尚不明确，因此需要构建相关数据集并进行系统验证。

Method: 构建NormasTCU数据集（巴西葡萄牙语法律IR数据集），然后使用三种LLM模型和两种提示技术对812个查询-文档对进行相关性评分，与人类标注的qrels对比，评估15个IR系统的排名，使用Cohen's kappa、Kendall's tau和bootstrap置信区间等指标进行分析。

Result: LLM在0-2评分尺度上存在0.46-0.66的Mean Absolute Error正向偏差；与人类判断的Cohen's kappa仅为0.32-0.53（fair到moderate水平）；在nDCG@10和MRR下系统排名与参考排名高度相似（Kendall's tau≥0.90），但在P@10和R@10下可靠性较低；LLM生成排名有时比单个人类标注者与参考排名的相关性更强。

Conclusion: LLM可以在使用nDCG或MRR等排序感知指标时有效支持巴西葡萄牙语专业语料库的可扩展相关性评估，但在依赖精确率或召回率时应避免使用LLM判断。

Abstract: Portuguese Information Retrieval (IR) lacks public datasets, and relevance assessment for specialized collections remains costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly support relevance assessment, their reliability in non-English specialized domains remains unclear. We introduce NormasTCU (https://huggingface.co/datasets/LeandroRibeiro/NormasTCU), a Brazilian Portuguese IR dataset with 14,469 legal documents, 46 queries, and 3,048 human judgments over 812 query-document pairs. Using NormasTCU, we evaluated LLM-as-a-judge for relevance assessment by prompting three models with two prompt techniques to grade these pairs. We then compared the rankings of 15 IR systems derived from LLM-generated and human reference qrels. LLMs consistently showed a positive scoring bias (mean absolute error: 0.46--0.66 on a 0-2 scale). Furthermore, pair-level agreement with human judgments achieved only fair to moderate levels, with Cohen's kappa ranging from 0.32 to 0.53. Despite this bias, LLM-generated judgments often yielded highly similar system rankings for nDCG@10 and MRR (observed Kendall's tau greater than or equal 0.90, although the bootstrap confidence intervals did not always remain above this threshold), but were less reliable for P@10 and R@10. Notably, LLM-based rankings were sometimes more strongly correlated with the reference ranking than individual human annotations were. As a practical implication, our results suggest that LLMs could effectively support scalable relevance assessment in specialized Portuguese corpora when evaluated using nDCG or MRR (rank-aware metrics), but they should be avoided when relying on precision or recall.

</details>


### [5] [LINE Conversation History Retrieval for Personal Memory RAG: Evaluating Search Representations and Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27809)
*Akito Hattori*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究针对个人记忆中基于检索增强生成（RAG）的初始探索，使用单一用户的358,896条LINE聊天记录进行仅检索案例研究。将消息分割为22,329个时间连贯的块，构建三种检索表示（原文、摘要、嵌入文本）。在100个评估问题上比较BM25、稠密向量检索和线性混合检索，最终选择的embedding_text_bm25与embedding_text_vector混合（beta=0.45）取得Recall@5=0.697，较最佳单一检索器提升0.113。但研究存在探索性局限：单用户、单标注者、配置选择与评估使用同一问题集，不涉及答案生成与泛化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在个人记忆检索增强生成方面尚未得到充分研究，本研究旨在作为该方向的初步探索，检验在个人聊天记录这种非正式、长尾的个人记忆场景下，不同检索策略的有效性。

Method: 将358,896条聊天消息按时间一致性分割为22,329个块，为每个块构建三种搜索表示（raw_text、生成的summary、结合摘要与原文摘录及固定文本的embedding_text）。使用BM25、稠密向量检索和线性混合检索三类检索器，在100个由单一标注者验证的评估问题上进行对比。探索6种检索器配对和21组权重共126种配置，使用Recall@5、MRR@5和nDCG@5等指标评估，并通过分位数自助法计算置信区间。

Result: 单一检索器中embedding_text_bm25最优（Recall@5=0.584）。选定的混合配置（embedding_text_bm25与embedding_text_vector，beta=0.45）达到Recall@5=0.697、MRR@5=0.595、nDCG@5=0.575，比单一最佳检索器提升0.113（95%CI [0.048, 0.184]）。但与摘要混合检索在beta=0.50时差异仅0.050（95%CI [-0.013, 0.115]），无法确立显著差异。聚合类问题的点估计值较低，表明块级检索在处理跨时间、跨对话分布证据的任务时存在困难。

Conclusion: 混合检索（结合BM25与稠密向量）在个人聊天记录检索中优于单一检索器，但该结论受限于探索性研究设计：仅单一用户和标注者、配置搜索与评估使用同一问题集（存在过拟合风险且置信区间条件化）、未评估最终答案生成质量，也未测试对未见问题的泛化能力。未来需扩大研究规模、引入多标注者、使用独立验证集，并探索更精细的证据聚合策略以应对跨时间分布的查询。

Abstract: As an initial step toward personal memory retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for large language models (LLMs), this study presents a retrieval-only case study over one user's LINE conversation history. We segmented 358,896 messages into 22,329 temporally coherent chunks and constructed three search representations: raw_text, a generated summary, and embedding_text, which combines a summary with a raw-text excerpt and other fixed text. We compared BM25, dense vector retrieval, and linear hybrid retrieval on 100 evaluation questions verified by a single annotator. Among individual retrievers, embedding_text_bm25 achieved the highest point estimate, with Recall@5 of 0.584. We then explored six retriever pairings and 21 weights, for 126 configurations on the same evaluation set. The selected combination of embedding_text_bm25 and embedding_text_vector at beta = 0.45 achieved Recall@5 = 0.697, MRR@5 = 0.595, and nDCG@5 = 0.575. Its Recall@5 exceeded that of embedding_text_bm25 by 0.113, with a question-level paired percentile-bootstrap 95% confidence interval of [0.048, 0.184]. This interval is conditional on fixing the configuration selected on the same 100 questions and does not account for uncertainty from configuration selection or weight search. The difference from a summary-based hybrid at beta = 0.50 was 0.050, with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.013, 0.115], so no clear difference could be established. The 17 aggregate questions also yielded lower point estimates than the other question types, suggesting that flat chunk-level retrieval struggles when evidence is distributed across multiple times and conversations. This evaluation is an exploratory single-user, single-annotator study conducted on the same question set used for configuration search; it does not evaluate final answer generation or generalization to unseen questions.

</details>


### [6] [Personalized and Multi-View Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27826)
*Jaehyung Lim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Junyoung Kim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PMFRec是一种针对联邦推荐中冷启动物品场景的新方法，通过个性化表示生成器和全局多视角编码器，解决现有方法在个性化缺失、语义组合失败及训练通信效率低下三方面的结构性问题，在冷物品推荐、用户公平性、暖场景适应性和LDP鲁棒性上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐方法大多假设固定物品池，忽略了新物品持续到达的现实冷启动场景。在服务器无法访问客户端交互数据、客户端无法访问服务器专有物品属性特征的双重约束下，现有联邦冷启动推荐方法存在三大结构性缺陷：缺乏个性化、将异构语义强制映射到单一嵌入空间导致组合失败、以及显式对齐协作与属性表示带来的训练和通信效率低下。

Method: PMFRec包含两个核心组件：1) 个性化表示生成器，从属性特征生成用户特定的物品表示；2) 全局多视角编码器，采用物品自适应门控（item-adaptive gating）和正交性目标（orthogonality objective）来捕获互补的语义视角并减少跨视角冗余。此外，PMFRec将协作知识和属性知识融合为单一交换的物品表示，消除了对客户端显式正则化器的需求并减少通信开销。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，PMFRec在冷物品推荐方面持续优于强基线，并且进一步改善了用户级公平性、暖场景适应性和局部差分隐私（LDP）下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PMFRec有效解决了联邦冷启动推荐中的个性化、组合性和效率问题，为现实世界中持续到达新物品的联邦推荐场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated recommendation (FedRec) enables personalized modeling without centralizing users' interaction histories, but most existing methods assume a fixed item pool and thus overlook the practical cold-item setting where new items continuously arrive. Under the dual-sided constraint, where the server cannot access clients' interactions while clients cannot access the server's proprietary item attribute features, prior federated cold-start recommendation approaches suffer from three structural limitations: a lack of personalization, compositionality failure caused by forcing heterogeneous semantics into a single embedding space, and training- and communication-inefficiency arising from explicit alignment between separate collaborative and attribute representations. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized and Multi-view Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation (PMFRec). PMFRec learns a personalized representation generator to produce user-specific item representations from attribute features, and introduces a global multi-view encoder with item-adaptive gating and an orthogonality objective to capture complementary semantic views while reducing cross-view redundancy. In addition, PMFRec fuses collaborative and attribute knowledge into a single exchanged item representation, eliminating the need for an explicit client-side regularizer and reducing communication overhead. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that PMFRec consistently outperforms strong baselines in cold-item recommendation and further improves user-level fairness, warm-scenario adaptability, and robustness under Local Differential Privacy (LDP).

</details>


### [7] [ITER: Interaction-Aware Retrieval for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27912)
*Haodong Chen,Shuai Wang,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ITER，一种基于智能体交互感知的稠密检索器。与现有方法仅依赖当前子查询及其搜索结果不同，ITER利用智能体轨迹学习信号进行训练，将主问题、当前子查询和前置子查询共同编码为查询表示，并使用轨迹相对学习信号进行监督。在六个智能体骨干和三个模型家族上，ITER平均比现有方法LRAT提升7.5%（InfoSeek-Eval）和13.5%（BrowseComp-Plus），且跨智能体鲁棒性优于AgentIR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究（deep-research）智能体通过迭代搜索步骤回答复杂问题，但检索器训练仅依赖当前步骤的子查询和对应搜索结果作为训练信号，忽略了智能体先前交互中积累的大量信息（如主问题、前置子查询、已访问文档等），导致这些轨迹信息未被充分利用。

Method: ITER使用智能体轨迹学习信号训练稠密检索器：查询表示不仅包含当前子查询，还整合主问题和前置子查询；训练时利用从智能体交互中衍生的轨迹相对学习信号，包括将先前访问过的有用文档作为后续搜索中的冗余负样本（redundancy negatives）进行监督。

Result: 在六个智能体骨干、三个模型家族上，ITER相较现有轨迹训练检索器LRAT，在InfoSeek-Eval上平均提升7.5%，在BrowseComp-Plus上平均提升13.5%；跨智能体鲁棒性优于依赖外部LLM判断信号和智能体搜索前推理的AgentIR。消融实验表明主问题和前置子查询构成最稳健的查询表示，先前访问的有用文档作为冗余负样本提供最强的轨迹相对监督。

Conclusion: ITER通过挖掘和利用智能体完整的交互轨迹信息（而非仅当前步骤），显著提升了深度研究智能体的检索性能，证明了轨迹级学习信号在检索器训练中的价值，并展示了跨智能体的鲁棒泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep-research agents answer complex user questions through an iterative sequence of search steps, where the agent autonomously formulates sub-queries to retrieve the evidence needed at each stage. However, existing retriever training typically relies only on the sub-query and its corresponding search results at the current step as training signals, leaving the information accumulated from previous interactions largely underutilized.
  We introduce iter, an agent interaction-aware dense retriever trained using agent trajectory learning signals. iter represents each query by incorporating not only the current sub-query, but also the main question and preceding sub-queries, and is trained using trajectory-relative learning signals derived from the agent's interactions.
  Across six agent backbones from three model families, iter consistently outperforms the existing agent-trajectory-trained dense retriever, LRAT, achieving an average improvement of 7.5% on InfoSeek-Eval and 13.5% on BrowseComp-Plus. iter also demonstrates stronger cross-agent robustness than AgentIR, a deep-research retriever that relies on external LLM-judge signals and the agent's pre-search reasoning. Ablations further show that the main question and previous sub-queries provide the most robust query representation, while previously visited and useful documents, used as redundancy negatives in subsequent searches, provide the strongest trajectory-relative supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/ielab/ITER.

</details>


### [8] [Information-Guided Selective Modality-Interest Alignment for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27950)
*Wenze Ma,Chenyu Sun,Yanmin Zhu,Qiwen Gu,Xuhao Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AMUR框架，一种基于信息引导的选择性模态-兴趣对齐方法，用于多模态推荐，通过细化模态图结构和选择性对齐跨模态共享的兴趣相关语义来提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMRec方法在模态利用上存在对齐过程隐式或启发式的问题，缺乏明确的优化目标来筛选更匹配用户兴趣的模态信号。直接融合所有模态信息可能引入噪声，因为用户兴趣通常只与部分模态信号相关。

Method: AMUR包含两个核心步骤：(1) 面向用户行为细化模态图结构；(2) 选择性对齐跨模态共享的兴趣相关语义，同时保留模态特有的互补信息，从而增强与用户兴趣相关的模态信息并降低不相关信号的影响。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，AMUR相对于竞争基线方法表现出优越的性能。

Conclusion: 基于信息论视角的AMUR通过明确的选择性模态-兴趣对齐目标，有效提升多模态推荐中模态与用户兴趣的匹配度，同时保留模态特有的有用信息，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation (MMRec) aims to enhance recommendation performance by leveraging rich item content from multiple modalities. However, directly incorporating all modality information does not necessarily lead to better preference modeling, since user interests are often more related to a subset of modality signals, while other signals may be weakly aligned with user preferences or even introduce noise. Although recent MMRec methods improve modality utilization through invariant learning, attention mechanisms, graph refinement, or contrastive learning, their alignment processes are often implicit or heuristic and lack a clear objective for selecting modality signals that better match user interests.
  In this paper, we propose AMUR, an information-guided selective modality-interest alignment framework for multimodal recommendation. Inspired by an information-theoretic view, AMUR aims to enhance modality information that is more related to user interests while reducing the influence of less aligned signals. Specifically, AMUR first refines modality graph structures towards user behavior, and then selectively aligns shared interest-related semantics across modalities. This enables AMUR to improve modality-interest alignment while preserving useful modality-specific complementary information. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AMUR over competitive baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Wenze1/AMUR.

</details>


### [9] [HubMixer: Progressive Latent Hub Mixing for Parameter-Efficient Feature Interaction in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27991)
*Jie Zhou,Zixian Gong,Wenhao Li,Chang Liu,Enzhao Shen,Bo Liu,Xu Guo,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HubMixer，一种用于推荐系统中特征交互的参数高效潜在枢纽混合架构，通过"归纳-交互-读出"范式组织异构特征交互，在快手短视频招聘业务的在线A/B测试中实现了5.48%的简历提交转化率提升并已全面部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token-mixing的架构（如简化版自注意力）虽在硬件效率上表现优异，但推荐系统中的token本质上是异构的（如用户画像、物品属性、行为序列、上下文特征等），直接在所有原始异构token空间进行混合会导致参数低效，因为模型需要隐式发现哪些特征组应该交互以及交互如何路由。

Method: HubMixer引入一小组可学习的潜在枢纽（latent hubs）来组织特征交互，采用"归纳-交互-读出"（induction-interaction-readout）三段式范式：(1) 枢纽归纳阶段通过交叉注意力让潜在枢纽查询输入token，将异构token压缩为紧凑的潜在枢纽表示；(2) 枢纽交互阶段在更干净的潜在枢纽空间中进行高阶交互；(3) 标记条件化读出阶段让每个原始token从交互后的枢纽中选择性地读取信息，注入全局交互语义同时保留token级别的字段身份。

Result: 在工业推荐任务的离线实验中，HubMixer优于现有最先进模型（SOTA）。在快手短视频招聘业务的在线A/B测试中，简历提交转化率实现了统计学显著的5.48%提升，且HubMixer已全面部署到生产环境。

Conclusion: 通过在潜在枢纽空间中组织特征交互而非直接混合原始异构token，HubMixer实现了参数效率与交互能力的兼顾，验证了"先归纳压缩、再交互、后选择性读出"的范式在工业推荐系统中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Learning effective feature interactions is central to industrial recommendation and advertising ranking systems. Recent token-mixing architectures simplify self-attention with lightweight mixing operators, improving hardware efficiency and enabling large-scale deployment. However, recommendation tokens are fundamentally heterogeneous: user profiles, item attributes, behavioral sequences, context features, statistical signals, and business-side features live in different semantic spaces and interact in sparse, sample-specific patterns. Directly mixing all tokens in the raw heterogeneous token space may therefore be parameter-inefficient, as the model must implicitly discover which feature groups should interact and how such interactions should be routed. In the paper, we propose HubMixer, a parameter-efficient latent hub mixing architecture for feature interaction in recommendation. Instead of directly mixing raw feature tokens, HubMixer introduces a small set of learnable latent hubs to organize feature interactions through an `induction--interaction--readout` paradigm. First, hub induction summarizes heterogeneous tokens into compact latent hubs, where latent hubs query input tokens through cross-attention. Second, hub interaction performs high-order interaction in the cleaner latent hub space. Third, token-conditioned readout lets each original token selectively read from the interacted hubs, injecting global interaction semantics while preserving token-level field identity. Extensive offline experiments on industrial recommendation tasks show that HubMixer outperforms the SOTA models. Online A/B testing in the Kuaishou short-video recruitment business further shows a statistically significant 5.48% improvement in resume submission conversion rate, and HubMixer has been fully deployed in production.

</details>


### [10] [Every Article Deserves a Video: Contextual Video Matching for Digital Publishers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28359)
*Arnaud Corone,Brice Pierre de la Briere,Gladys Roch,Samuel Leonardo Gracio,Yassine Bouher,Parvati Chauchaix*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dailymotion开发的"上下文视频匹配"系统，利用大型语言模型和文本嵌入技术，自动将相关视频与文本型网页及文章进行匹配，解决了大规模出版商的视频嵌入难题，系统上线后已被数百家出版商采用，显著提升了用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字出版商面临管理海量内容目录的挑战，需要在基于文本的文章中有效嵌入相关视频以提升变现能力和用户留存。然而，人工选择在大规模场景下不切实际，尤其是面对出版商自身庞大的视频库或Dailymotion的全球目录时，迫切需要一种自动化的视频匹配解决方案。

Method: 该系统利用大型语言模型（LLMs）和文本嵌入技术，自动将相关视频与文本密集型网页和文章进行匹配。论文详细阐述了系统的动机、架构、评估方法及其在Dailymotion生产环境中的部署方案。

Result: 系统自上线以来已被数百家出版商采纳，显著提高了用户参与度，并通过高度相关的视频内容丰富了用户体验。

Conclusion: 基于LLM和文本嵌入的Contextual Video Matching系统为大规模出版商提供了一种可扩展的视频内容匹配解决方案，在实际生产环境中验证了其有效性和商业价值。

Abstract: As digital publishers face the challenge of managing massive content catalogs, the ability to effectively embed relevant video within text-based articles has become critical for both monetization and user retention. However, manual selection is impractical for large scale publishers, especially when navigating their own extensive video libraries or the entire global Dailymotion catalog. In this paper, we present the "Contextual Video Matching" system, a solution that automatically matches relevant videos with text-heavy web pages and articles. By leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and textual embeddings, we provide a scalable solution for publishers to efficiently combine video content with their articles. We discuss in detail the motivations, architecture, evaluations, and deployment of this system within Dailymotion's production environment. Since its launch, the system has been adopted by hundreds of publishers, significantly increasing user engagement and enriching user experiences with highly relevant video content.

</details>


### [11] [SG-UMP: Sequence-Guided Universal Multimodal Prioritization Calculation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28503)
*Xinyi Zhang,Yutong Li,Peijie Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SG-UMP，一种即插即用的通用多模态优先计算框架，通过Module Combiner和Module Router分别实现灵活的多模态处理和动态模块排序，从而同时适应用户级偏好异质性与数据集级模态偏差，在四个真实数据集上显著提升不同骨干网络和模态设置下的推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐（MSR）方法未能捕捉用户层面的偏好异质性以及数据集层面的模态偏差，导致模型在不同用户和不同数据集之间的适应能力有限。

Method: 提出SG-UMP框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Module Combiner，用于灵活的多模态信息处理；(2) Module Router，用于动态模块排序，从而同时适应用户偏好和数据集特征。该框架以即插即用（plug-and-play）插件形式工作。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SG-UMP在不同骨干网络和多模态设置下均能一致地提升推荐性能，并开源了代码。

Conclusion: SG-UMP作为一种通用插件框架，有效解决了MSR中用户级偏好异质性和数据集级模态偏差问题，具有较强的跨用户、跨数据集的适应性和实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommendation (MSR) improves recommendation by incorporating heterogeneous information such as text, images, and user interactions. However, existing MSR methods often fail to capture user-level preference heterogeneity and dataset-level modality bias, limiting their adaptability across users and datasets. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{S}equence-\textbf{G}uided \textbf{U}niversal \textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{P}rioritization Calculation Framework (\textbf{SG-UMP}), a plug-and-play plugin for enhancing multimodal information processing in MSR. SG-UMP includes a Module Combiner for flexible multimodal processing and a Module Router for dynamic module ordering, enabling adaptation to both user preferences and dataset characteristics. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that SG-UMP consistently improves recommendation performance across different backbones and multimodal settings. The code is available at https://github.com/esemsc-xz524/SG-UMP .

</details>


### [12] [QUEST: A Query and Extraction System for Topics in Asylum Law Application Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28555)
*Maria Vlachou,Anna Murphy Høgenhaug,Mohammad N. S. Jahromi,Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Thomas Gammeltoft-Hansen,Thomas B. Moeslund,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QUEST系统，一个用于从丹麦庇护申请上诉文件中提取和识别可信度评估因素的系统。该系统将问题构建为信息检索任务，结合合成查询生成、主题提取和相关性评估，并提出了新的领域特定评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 庇护申请的法律裁决文件通常冗长、复杂且异构，涵盖叙述性申请人访谈、原始决定和补充材料。当申请被拒绝时，处理上诉的关键问题在于：原始申请中的信息可信度是否是一个决定性因素。然而，从复杂文档中自动提取与可信度评估相关的因素是一项具有挑战性的任务。

Method: QUEST系统采用信息检索框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 合成查询生成，自动生成与可信度相关的查询；(2) 主题提取，从上诉材料中提取关键主题；(3) 相关性评估，识别与可信度指标相关的信息。此外，作者提出了一种新型的领域特定评估指标，不同于传统的相关性评估，专门用于评估系统在可信度因素上的表现。

Result: 实验结果揭示了在使用基于可信度的相关性评估时存在更大的挑战，表明该任务难度较高。系统在不同类型的可信度指标上表现有所差异，研究获得了关于自动方法在处理不同类型庇护上诉可信度指标时的能力的洞察。

Conclusion: 基于可信度的相关性评估比传统评估更具挑战性，这凸显了庇护申请可信度评估任务的复杂性。QUEST系统为该领域提供了一个可行的自动化框架，但其性能仍需进一步提升。

Abstract: Legal decisions on asylum applications consist of long, complex, and heterogeneous documents, covering narrative applicant interviews, original decisions, and additional supporting materials. If an application is rejected, a critical question in processing an appeal is whether the credibility of the information in the original application was a factor that determined the original decision. In this paper, we present the QUEST system (Query and Extraction System for Topics) to extract and identify factors relating to credibility assessments in two datasets of Danish asylum application appeals. QUEST frames this problem as an information retrieval task, combining synthetic query generation, topic extraction, and relevance assessment to identify information related to credibility indicators in appeals board application materials. In addition to standard retrieval evaluation metrics, we propose a new type of domain-specific assessments distinct from the traditional relevance to evaluate the performance of the tested systems with respect to credibility factors. In this way, we obtain insights about how well automatic methods can return answers for different types of indicators appearing in asylum appeals. Our results indicate that there is an increased challenge when estimating performance using credibility-based relevance assessments, thus pointing to the difficulty of the task.

</details>


### [13] [PULSAR: Pooled Unified Late-Interaction Search and Retrieval for Enterprise Visual Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28572)
*Benjamin Constable,Anup Roy,Vishal Sharma,Rishabh Upadhyay,Robin Mills,Aidan Millar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PULSAR是一个部署于Mubadala投资公司的生产级视觉优先检索系统。它采用冻结的ColPali风格骨干网络对页面图片进行索引，利用两阶段池化晚期交互索引实现高效检索。相比未池化配置，向量搜索延迟降低15.1倍，生产环境延迟为156ms，QPS提升约88倍，且每页索引成本较OCR+文本化方案低约20倍。自2026年3月起已服务超过3000笔交易中的78,000份文档、约240万个页面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构投资者处理的融资文件（如Pitch Deck、董事会材料、尽调文件）视觉密度高，且在交易接近结束时每小时都会变化。传统OCR+图表文本化的方案规模刷新成本高昂，且可能丢失图表细节，亟需一种更高效且保留视觉信息的检索方案。

Method: PULSAR采用视觉优先的检索路线：使用冻结的ColPali风格骨干网络对页面图像进行索引，并采用两阶段池化晚期交互索引设计——紧凑的页面摘要支持初步检索，随后使用更细粒度的池化表示进行精确MaxSim重排序，兼顾检索速度与精度。

Result: 在ViDoRe V3基准上，池化设计将中位向量搜索延迟降低15.1倍，NDCG@10和Recall@10的绝对损失小于0.01；生产环境中位延迟为156ms，并发负载下QPS约为未池化索引的88倍。事件驱动的索引路径每页成本比OCR+文本化方案低约20倍。在生产Top K下，答案事实召回率比OCR+文本化基线提升了一倍以上。系统已服务78,000份文档和约240万个页面，覆盖3,000多笔交易。

Conclusion: PULSAR验证了视觉优先检索在生产级机构投资场景中的可行性与优越性：在保持检索精度的前提下大幅降低延迟和成本，同时显著提升答案事实召回率，是替代传统OCR+文本化管线的高效方案。

Abstract: Institutional investors search visually dense pitch decks, board packs, and diligence materials that change hourly near deal closing. OCR followed by figure verbalisation is costly to refresh at this scale and can lose chart detail. We present PULSAR, a production vision-first retrieval system deployed at Mubadala Investment Company. PULSAR indexes page images with a frozen ColPali-style backbone and uses a pooled two-stage late-interaction index: compact page summaries support initial retrieval, followed by exact MaxSim rescoring over a finer pooled representation. On ViDoRe V3, this design reduces median vector-search latency by 15.1 times against an unpooled configuration with less than 0.01 absolute NDCG@10 and Recall@10 loss; production median vector-search latency is 156 ms. Under concurrent load, the pooled index sustains approximately 88 times higher QPS than an unpooled index. The event-driven ingestion path is estimated to be approximately 20 times cheaper per page than the OCR+verbalisation baseline it replaced. Since March 2026, PULSAR has served 78 thousand documents and approximately 2.4 million pages across more than 3,000 deals. At the production top K, it more than doubles answer-fact recall over the OCR+verbalisation baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [14] [QGPINNs: A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Nonlocal Differential Equations on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28589)
*Vaibhav Mehandiratta,Saket Ramchandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了QGPINNs，一个基于PyTorch的物理信息神经网络框架，用于求解量子图上的非局部微分方程，支持分数阶椭圆问题和时间分数阶演化方程，并可扩展到逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子图上的非局部（分数阶）微分方程在工程和物理中有广泛应用，但缺乏通用且高效的数值求解框架。传统的图神经网络和PINN方法难以处理量子图上复杂的顶点传输条件与分数阶非局部算子。

Method: QGPINNs将每条边上的解用独立神经网络近似，通过统一的基于图的损失函数约束控制方程、初始/边界条件和顶点传输条件（连续性条件与Kirchhoff-Neumann条件）。集成了软/硬约束施加、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入以及可学习的奇异性捕获特征等策略，并扩展到分数算子阶数和物理参数的逆问题识别。

Result: 在基准图结构和真实网络（IEEE 14节点系统、农业排水网络）上的数值实验验证了该框架的准确性、计算效率和物理一致性。

Conclusion: QGPINNs为量子图上的非局部微分方程提供了一种通用的、可扩展到逆问题的深度学习求解框架，在真实网络应用上展示了良好的性能。

Abstract: We propose QGPINNs, a physics-informed neural network framework developed in PyTorch for the numerical solution of nonlocal differential equations on quantum graphs. The framework is designed as a general computational implementation in which the solution on each edge of the graph is approximated by a neural network, while a unified graph-based loss function enforces the governing equations together with initial, boundary, and vertex transmission conditions. In particular, the formulation incorporates standard continuity and Kirchhoff-Neumann vertex conditions and Dirichlet boundary conditions into the learning process to couple the local edge-wise neural approximations into a global solution on the graph. The framework is developed for two representative classes of nonlinear models: multi-order fractional elliptic problems and time-fractional evolution equations on quantum graphs. To improve accuracy and training stability, QGPINNs integrates several graph-adapted learning strategies, including soft and hard constraint enforcement, dynamic loss balancing, Fourier feature embeddings, and a learnable singularity-capturing feature for weakly singular solutions arising in the considered problems. The framework also extends naturally to inverse problems, including the identification of the orders of fractional operators and physical parameters from noisy observational data. We validate the accuracy, computational efficiency, and physical consistency of the proposed framework through numerical experiments on benchmark graph structures and real-world networks, including the IEEE 14-bus system and an open-channel agricultural drainage network.

</details>


### [15] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过leave-one-policy-out覆盖度与状态所有者专业化估计策略特定信用，在保持PGPSE池化目标的同时按信用分配非负辅助内在奖励，以促进并行策略间的互补覆盖。在多个基准测试中一致提升归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE通过训练独立参数化的策略在相同环境的多个副本中扩展状态空间覆盖，但其池化团队熵分数仅衡量集体探索，无法识别贡献非冗余覆盖的具体策略，缺乏策略层面的信用分配机制。

Method: 提出Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE)：将leave-one-policy-out覆盖度与状态所有者专业化相结合，估计策略特定信用；保持PGPSE的池化目标不变，将非负辅助内在奖励按信用非均匀重新分配（保持总质量守恒），以抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。

Result: 在受控环境、7个公开离散状态基准以及PGPSE原始协议的Room和Maze设置中，MCC-PGPSE在所有测试场景下均相对Entropy基线产生正的归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度的末窗口增益。受控任务对比与固定基准集聚合结果显著，五种子原始协议对比方向一致。消融与信用对齐控制表明主要增益来自leave-one-policy-out覆盖度而非非均匀加权、错配信用或纯神经新颖性。

Conclusion: 贡献条件化的辅助奖励分配是提升离散状态空间中并行策略互补覆盖的一种可解释且有效的方法。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [16] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示后训练量化并非语义中立的优化过程，而是可能引发模型行为剧变的潜在安全漏洞。作者通过形式化"量化行为等价类"（QBECs）证明量化映射不保证行为等价性，并提出三阶段对抗微调框架，在INT8或4-bit压缩时激活恶意行为，在机器翻译和立场分类两个实际场景中验证了该攻击的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM边缘部署流程存在"验证-部署鸿沟"：模型在FP16源精度下通过安全认证，但量化后未经重新评估就直接部署。量化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度的安全认证无法保证量化后模型的行为等价性，这为"量化触发后门攻击"提供了理论基础。

Method: 1) 形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBECs），并证明QBEC成员关系不蕴含行为等价性；2) 提出三阶段对抗微调框架，在满足源精度检查的前提下将恶意载荷嵌入模型，使其在INT8或4-bit量化压缩时激活攻击行为；3) 在战术机器翻译和多语言编解码器序列到序列模型上验证，并扩展到政治内容分析立场分类场景；4) 进行跨量化器迁移性分析。

Result: 后门翻译模型在FP16修复后"敌友腐败"为零，但量化后反转率高达85.02%；立场分类器在压缩后意识形态偏移达ΔBias=0.33。跨量化器分析表明攻击持久性由量化方案和模型架构共同决定，而非仅由名义位宽决定。

Conclusion: 仅依赖源精度审计无法排除量化触发的恶意行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证流程，才能保障边缘AI的可信性。量化操作应被视为需要同等安全审查的部署环节，而非语义中立的优化手段。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [17] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究基于Gated DeltaNet (GDN)和Kimi Delta Attention (KDA)的语言模型中循环状态的训练后量化问题。提出的DAMP方法利用量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性来识别高风险通道，以高精度存储这些通道，其余通道用INT8存储。在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上评估显示，DAMP在9.9 bits/状态值时保持接近FP32基线的精度，将循环状态存储降低69.1%，加速循环状态更新内核达2.01x，降低全模型TPOT达10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GDN和KDA的语言模型通过固定大小的循环状态替代KV缓存来降低推理内存，但这些循环状态通常以FP32存储，占用大量GPU内存且更新受限于内存带宽，显著拖慢解码延迟。然而，目前尚无人研究这类循环状态的后训练量化问题，因此需要一种能在精度和存储之间取得更好平衡的量化方案。

Method: 首先分析发现均匀量化对循环状态效果不佳：INT8和FP8已导致复杂推理任务精度退化，INT4和NVFP4几乎使精度降为零。进一步分析发现量化误差能量集中在少数通道中，且状态通道的相对衰减强度在不同提示和任务间保持稳定。基于这些发现，DAMP在离线校准阶段同时利用量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性来识别高风险通道，对这些通道以更高精度存储，其余通道使用INT8。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B两个模型上，覆盖数学推理、一般推理和代码生成六个基准的评估中，DAMP在9.9 bits/状态值时保持接近FP32基线的平均精度。循环状态存储降低69.1%，循环状态更新内核加速最高达2.01倍，全模型TPOT降低最高达10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP通过通道级混合精度量化，有效解决了GDN和KDA语言模型中循环状态的存储和带宽瓶颈，在不显著牺牲精度的前提下大幅降低存储开销并加速推理，为高效部署此类长上下文语言模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [18] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了块稀疏特征化器（BSF）的优缺点，发现其仍存在特征分裂和组合等经典SAE失效模式，提出了包括Tournament Top-K选择规则在内的多项架构改进以缓解这些问题，并将块范式扩展到交叉编码器（crosscoder）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF作为近期提出的与SAE类似的工具，以子空间（方向块）而非单个方向为原子单元，旨在捕捉视觉中常见的低维流形特征。但该工作尚未系统研究其局限性，也不清楚其是否继承了SAE经典失效模式。

Method: 本文通过实验系统分析BSF的优势与缺陷，确认其仍受特征分裂、特征组合等SAE经典问题困扰；提出多项架构改进，其中核心创新是Tournament Top-K选择规则以显著减少特征分裂；并将块范式扩展至crosscoder设置。

Result: 研究发现BSF仍存在经典SAE故障模式；Tournament Top-K选择规则能显著降低特征分裂问题；块范式成功扩展至crosscoder中。

Conclusion: BSF虽针对低维流形特征设计，但仍有SAE固有缺陷；所提出的Tournament Top-K等架构改动有效缓解了这些问题，且块范式可推广到crosscoder，为后续可解释性研究提供了改进方向。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [19] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了持续学习（CL）和模型合并（MM）中灾难性遗忘与权重解耦误差本质上是同一现象——"任务干扰"（task interference），并将其形式化为层间Frobenius内积<ΔW_l, J_l(x)>_F。作者从理论上推导出上界，证明谱范数||ΔW_l||_2是优化器可控的干扰因子，并发现Muon优化器从构造上调节该因子。实验表明，用Muon替换AdamW在八任务模型合并基准上最高提升+5.02点，在持续学习的多个协议上也取得一致的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习和模型合并虽然目标相同（获得一个跨多任务表现良好的单一模型），但两者的核心困难——灾难性遗忘和权重解耦误差——在文献中一直被分开处理。作者认为这两个困难实际上是同一现象的两种表现：一个任务的参数更新会改变模型在另一个任务上的输出。此外，已有的解决方案忽略了基础优化器引发的几何结构，作者希望揭示优化器在这一共享现象中的关键作用。

Method: 作者提出"任务干扰"（task interference）概念将其形式化为层间Frobenius内积<ΔW_l, J_l(x)>_F。理论推导出上界，将谱范数||ΔW_l||_2隔离为优化器可控制的干扰因子，并通过逐模式（per-mode）分析证明该上界能捕捉经验干扰的主导部分。进而识别出Muon优化器能从构造上调节该因子。

Result: 理论分析得到实验充分验证：用Muon替换AdamW在八任务模型合并基准、三个CLIP骨干网络上最高提升+5.02点准确率；在持续学习方面，Muon在十个类增量协议、三个任务增量协议以及十一任务MTIL基准上均取得一致的正面收益。

Conclusion: CL和MM的困难可统一为任务干扰现象，其核心可归因于优化器可控的谱范数因子。Muon优化器因其对谱范数的优雅控制，收紧了两类任务的干扰上界，成为一种可与现有解决方案互补的、以优化器为中心的通用方法。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [20] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dandelion，一种球面版本的warp-based神经PDE求解器（Flower），通过预测切平面位移和沿大圆传输特征实现空间混合，无需卷积操作，并在层次化池化中完全使用球谐域实现。作者同时发布了一个包含多种天然球面PDE数据集的基准套件，实验表明Dandelion在所有数据集上表现最佳或次佳，且与非线性warp基线相比优势随分辨率增大而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学过程在球面上展开，但现有科学机器学习架构默认基于欧几里得空间，直接应用于经纬网格会产生高纬度畸变、错误的双周期性假设和球面测地距离失真等问题。虽已有球面卷积、球面傅里叶神经算子和测地线注意力等原生球面方法，但仍缺乏一种高效且统一的球面神经PDE求解器，以及一个规模适中、具有挑战性的球面基准数据集。

Method: Dandelion的核心思路是将Flower（基于warp的神经PDE求解器）扩展到球面：每一层网络预测一个切平面位移，并沿大圆（测地线）传输特征，从而通过球面坐标变换实现空间混合，完全不依赖卷积。在U-Net式结构中，层次化池化全部在球谐域中实现。同时作者发布了一个涵盖多种挑战性PDE问题的球面基准套件，包括修改的Galewsky急流、异常链式湍流、Cahn-Hilliard分解、球面Riemann激波、Held-Suarez干大气输运和全球海洋动力学。

Result: Dandelion在基准套件的所有数据集上均取得最佳或第二佳的性能。在高分辨率（256×512）下单步预测和滚动预测中，Dandelion与Flower2D占据前两名。与非线性warp之外的其他基线相比，性能差距随分辨率提高而扩大。

Conclusion: 基于warp的球面方法Dandelion在多个天然球面PDE任务上优于或媲美现有的球面卷积、球面FNO和测地线注意力等方法，且高分辨率下优势更明显，表明"无卷积、仅通过球面坐标变换实现空间混合"这一思路在球面科学机器学习中具有广阔前景。作者发布的基准套件填补了现有球面数据集在规模与挑战性之间的空白。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [21] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEMGNN，一种端到端自解释的多标签图神经网络，能同时完成多标签节点分类并识别每个节点相对于预测标签的关键边（证据），利用标签间相关性提升分类性能和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多标签图学习方法缺乏训练时的可解释性；虽然已有事后（post-hoc）图解释器，但它们未显式建模标签依赖的证据共享，尤其在标签对弱相关或负相关时，无法准确刻画不同标签间证据应共享还是分离的问题，导致解释不够忠实。

Method: SEMGNN在同一框架和训练目标中联合学习一个预测器和一个稀疏边掩码解释器，利用标签-标签相关性来改进多标签节点分类并增强每个标签的解释，使得节点不同标签可由不同但连贯的结构和/或相关证据支持。

Result: 在社交网络、娱乐和生命科学领域的合成及真实多标签网络上，SEMGNN在保持竞争性或更优预测性能的同时，提供了更忠实和紧凑的标签条件化解释。

Conclusion: 端到端的自解释设计比事后解释方法更能捕捉多标签学习中标签依赖的证据共享/分离关系，在预测性能和解释质量上均具有优势。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [22] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARSANN，一种基于局部流形曲率的自适应最近邻分类框架。它通过TwoNN估计内在维度、PCA构建内在表示、shape-operator估计局部平均曲率，并据此自适应收缩邻域的径向范围——高曲率区域收缩更强，平坦区域保持更宽的空间支持。在70多个OpenML真实数据集上，CARSANN一致优于标准k-NN，且在45个受控数据集中的40个上提升了平衡准确率（0.6506→0.7528），统计检验确认显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-NN在整个特征空间施加相同的邻域基数（k值），但当数据局部几何结构在不同流形区域变化显著时，这一假设不充分。现有自适应方法仅调整邻居数量或局部度量，未能显式适应局部证据的空间范围。论文旨在引入局部流形曲率作为几何控制变量，自适应调整邻域的空间支持范围。

Method: CARSANN包含三个步骤：(1) 使用TwoNN估计内在维数，并通过PCA构建数据的低维内在表示；(2) 基于shape-operator公式估计局部平均曲率；(3) 曲率控制邻域尺度：高曲率区域施加更强的径向收缩，近似平坦区域保留更宽的空间支持。与仅修改邻居数量或局部度量的方法不同，CARSANN显式调整局部证据的空间范围。

Result: 在超过70个真实OpenML数据集上，CARSANN一致优于标准k-NN并与自适应最近邻方法竞争。受控比较中（同一基础邻域大小），CARSANN在45个数据集中的40个上获得更高平衡准确率，平均平衡准确率从0.6506提升至0.7528。对固定k=5的k-NN优势依然存在，Friedman与Nemenyi检验确认改进在统计上显著。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可以有效作为几何控制变量来适应邻域支持范围，提供了一种与基于基数的最近邻自适应互补的新范式。CARSANN验证了通过感知局部几何复杂度来调整空间支持的有效性和统计显著性。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [23] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将刺激几何的自同构群（特别是Demetci等人发现的廉价近重复置换不变性）转化为设计阶段的诊断与干预工具，通过一个低成本检测（一次函数调用），在数据收集前识别并规避表征对齐的退化风险。在颜色刺激上，即使将重启预算提高64倍，对称设计仍无法对齐，而相同样本量下的非对称集合每次都能恢复。仅基于这一诊断选择九种颜色，无需参考任何学习表征，即可将所有93个模型表征移离退化点，将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐仅从几何信息恢复刺激间一一对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群从根本上限制了任何对齐方法所能识别的对应关系，且这种退化在数据存在之前就已注定。现有的最廉价非恒等重标号诊断方法未能正确排序两个已发表的设计，因为密集采样产生的近重复刺激的置换代价几乎为零，导致诊断失效。作者旨在将该已知不变性转化为一种设计阶段的诊断工具，在数据收集之前检测并干预这种结构性退化风险。

Method: 作者将已知的近重复置换不变性（Demetci等，2024）转化为设计阶段的可操作诊断。利用颜色刺激中候选几何具有闭式解的优势进行理论分析，并通过大规模实验（含64倍重启预算的对照、3,000个子集的相关性分析）验证失败的结构性本质。最终提出一个仅需一次函数调用的设计前检查步骤，用于检测规则设计（如均匀间隔的朝向、音调、运动方向）与其候选几何的等距群之间的风险。

Result: 实验表明：1) 退化是结构性的——64倍重启预算下对称设计仍无法对齐，而非对称设计在相同N下总能恢复；2) 区分表征模型与恢复对应关系是几乎不相关的目标（r = -0.02，3,000个子集）；3) 仅使用该诊断选择九种颜色，无需参考任何学习表征，就能将所有93个模型表征移离退化点，并将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%，且模型、层数、N和求解器均保持不变。

Conclusion: 刺激几何的对称性是表征对齐任务中一种结构性、可预测的退化来源，影响范围覆盖所有规则刺激设计（均匀朝向、音调、运动方向等），而该风险可以在数据收集之前通过一次函数调用即可检测。这为神经科学和机器学习中的实验设计提供了一个重要的设计阶段检查工具，无需依赖数据或已学习的表征即可进行干预。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [24] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生长自组织映射（GSOM）的生成式持续学习框架，通过分布统计记忆（每个GSOM单元维护均值和协方差估计）和编码器-解码器模型实现无示例回放（exemplar-free replay），无需存储原始数据。该方法完全无监督，不依赖任务边界或类别标签，在多个基准上取得了与监督类记忆方法相当甚至更优的性能，并在单类增量场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习面临灾难性遗忘的核心挑战，现有方法通常依赖存储原始样本（memory-based）或需要任务边界和类标签（supervised），这限制了方法的通用性和扩展性。作者旨在开发一种无需存储原始数据、无需监督信息、同时具备竞争性能的持续学习方案，以克服现有方法的局限性。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 使用GSOM作为自组织增长拓扑结构，用于适应不断到达的新类别；(2) 每个GSOM单元维护自身的均值、方差和协方差分布统计量，构成"分布统计记忆"；(3) 通过从这些分布中进行祖先采样（ancestral sampling）生成合成样本，替代存储原始数据；(4) 在编码器-解码器配置中，生成的样本先经过编码再解码回输入空间用于后续训练；(5) 整个训练过程完全无监督，无需任务边界或类别标签。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准上，该方法性能与监督式的、基于记忆的现有最先进方法相当，同时持续优于免记忆（memory-free）方法。在具有挑战性的单类增量学习场景中，该方法匹配或超越了现有基线。此外，论文还提供了单类增量场景下TinyImageNet和MiniImageNet的基线结果，为未来研究提供参考。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了无监督的、自适应的、拓扑驱动的神经形式统计回放可以作为持续学习中一种可扩展且灵活的有效方法。通过分布统计记忆替代原始数据存储，在保持高性能的同时免除了存储开销和标签依赖，为持续学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [25] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SegBench-GC，一个受控压力测试基准，用以评估离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中人为管理性轨迹分割（与真实终止无关）对多步目标学习的影响。通过引入续延有效目标（CVT）作为分割一致性对照，在PoinMaze和Puzzle-4x5任务上定量展示了朴素分割处理可导致成功率从50.5%骤降至19.1%，而CVT保持接近未分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线GCRL常依赖轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步target学习，但实际记录轨迹可能因管理原因被分割（如日志分段），这些分割并不对应真实任务终止。现有方法对此缺乏系统的受控评估，导致这类分割如何影响多步离线GCRL的性能尚不清晰。

Method: 构建SegBench-GC基准：在保持转移数据、源轨迹、目标采样、优化设置和评估完全一致的条件下，仅变化人工备份边界（artificial backup boundaries）及其是否保留续延价值。引入CVT作为分割一致性控制——在人工切割点停止累积奖励，但target从存储的后继状态进行bootstrap。在PointMaze中进行匹配计数的研究（35,000个人工切割、三种分割实现、三种优化种子），并对Puzzle-4x5使用独立发布的n-step基线（n=25, Decoupled Q-Chunking代码库）进行交叉验证。

Result: PointMaze上最终50 episode/task成功率：未分割50.5%、CVT 39.1%、朴素吸收19.1%；不同分割实现下朴素平均成功率从4.8%到31.9%波动。Puzzle-4x5上：未分割47.2%、CVT 58.5%、朴素0.27%。target级诊断验证分析target差异达到数值精度；学习critic诊断显示朴素处理导致大幅乐观偏移，而CVT与未分割critic近似对齐。

Conclusion: CVT采用标准续延bootstrap而非新的Bellman规则，本工作贡献在于受控基准、失效隔离以及跨学习器的证据，表明管理性分割可能实质性改变多步离线GCRL的性能。朴素分割处理会引入系统性偏差，而CVT可作为分割不变性的有效对照方案。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor. In a matched-count PointMaze study with 35,000 artificial cuts, three segmentation realizations, and three optimization seeds, final 50-episode-per-task success is 50.5% uncut, 39.1% with CVT, and 19.1% when the same cuts are treated as absorbing; across segmentation realizations, naive mean success ranges from 4.8% to 31.9%. An independent published n-step baseline (n=25) from the Decoupled Q-Chunking codebase shows the same failure on Puzzle-4x5: 47.2% uncut, 58.5% CVT, and 0.27% naive across three optimization seeds. A target-level diagnostic verifies the analytic target difference to numerical precision, and learned-critic diagnostics show a large optimistic shift under naive handling while CVT remains approximately aligned with the uncut critic. CVT applies standard continuation bootstrapping rather than a new Bellman rule; the contribution is the controlled benchmark, failure isolation, and cross-learner evidence that administrative segmentation can materially change multi-step offline GCRL.

</details>


### [26] [SafeStep: An Interactive Demonstration of Semantic Communication for Pedestrian Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27688)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Jeremiah Yang,Sirin Chakraborty,Samuel Chamoun,Travis Ross,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeStep是一个基于浏览器的交互式语义通信平台，用于实时行人安全监控。该平台从四个实时交通摄像头提取行人信息，通过语义通信收发器经AWGN信道传输，并为每个用户渲染位置、轨迹和风险标签。平台支持独立选择收发器、SNR、码长和AoI等参数，并首次使AoI引起的下游性能退化在实时监控应用中直接可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信研究大多停留在仿真层面，缺乏面向实际应用的可交互实时平台。此外，Age of Information（AoI）对下游任务性能的影响在实时场景中尚未被直观展示，因此需要构建一个端到端的实时语义通信演示平台来桥接理论与实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: SafeStep平台架构包括：四个实时交通摄像头的行人信息提取模块、基于语义通信的收发器（AWGN信道）、以及面向每个浏览器的独立参数配置和可视化模块。平台支持独立选择收发器、SNR、码长和AoI参数，并将名为Meta-VIB的语义通信设计（仅4.16百万参数）与五种基线收发器进行对比。Meta-VIB通过紧凑的神经模型在不同SNR、码长和AoI条件下泛化，无需在线重训练。

Result: 实验结果表明：Meta-VIB相比基线平均任务损失降低最高达92.1%；在高性能GPU服务器上，集成并发访问负载支持20个用户保持5帧/秒的目标帧率；在100个用户各自请求不同配置时，零请求失败且平均应用响应时间低于1秒，但每浏览器平均帧率降至约1帧/秒。

Conclusion: SafeStep是首个在实时监控应用中使AoI诱导的下游性能退化直接可观测的实时语义通信平台，证明了语义通信在实际部署中的可行性，并为后续研究提供了可交互的验证工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop SafeStep, an interactive browser-based semantic communication platform for live pedestrian safety monitoring. SafeStep extracts pedestrian information from four live traffic-camera feeds, transmits it through a semantic communication transceiver over an Additive White Gaussian Noise (AWGN) channel, and renders user-specific positions, trajectories, and risk labels. The platform allows to independently select the transceiver, Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), codelength, and Age of Information (AoI), and demonstrates the transceiver performance of the selected configuration through live pedestrian safety monitoring to each browser. SafeStep compares a recently proposed semantic communication design called Meta-VIB with five baseline transceivers. Meta-VIB uses a compact neural model with only $4.16$ million parameters to generalize across varying SNR, codelength, and AoI values without online retraining. Experimental results show that Meta-VIB achieves mean task-loss reductions of up to $92.1\%$. On one high-end GPU server, the integrated concurrent-access workload maintains the target $5$ frames/s through $20$ users. At $100$ users, each requesting a distinct configuration, SafeStep records no request failures and a mean application response time below $1$ s, but its mean per-browser frame rate falls to approximately $1$ frame/s. To our knowledge, SafeStep is the first real-time semantic communication platform to make AoI-induced downstream degradation directly observable in live monitoring applications.

</details>


### [27] [RiskBlend: A Multi-Signal Framework for Test Input Prioritization in Machine Learning Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27704)
*Madhusudan Srinivasan,Namith Nishal Raphae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiskBlend框架，通过融合四种跨版本行为信号（历史失败模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移、邻域变化）来优先排序机器学习回归故障检测的测试输入，在1200个实验配置中全面超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分类器重训练后会产生回归故障（先前正确分类的输入被新版本错误分类），这些故障的检测成本高昂（需要人工标注、专家审查或昂贵仿真）。现有的测试输入优先排序方法主要依赖单一模型的置信度分数，未能充分利用两个模型版本之间在预测、决策边界和局部邻域上的变化信息。

Method: RiskBlend是一个分类器无关的优先排序框架，组合四种互补的风险信号：（1）历史失败模式、（2）预测偏移、（3）决策边界偏移、（4）邻域变化。这些信号通过验证集上学习的APFD-squared权重进行融合，以最大化回归故障检测效率。

Result: 在4个数据集、5种分类器、4种回归更新场景和15个随机种子下，共1200个实验配置中，RiskBlend在全部80个数据集-分类器-场景组合中取得最高平均APFD，相比最强基线提升最高达0.32 APFD。置信度方法仅在稀疏类别特征的线性分类器上具有竞争力，归因于特征空间几何特性。

Conclusion: 跨版本行为信号为机器学习系统中的回归故障优先排序提供了重要的互补信息，RiskBlend证明融合多种变化信号比依赖单一置信度分数更有效。

Abstract: When machine learning classifiers are retrained, inputs correctly classified by the previous model version may be misclassified by the updated version, creating regression faults that are costly to detect because verifying predictions against ground truth may require human annotation, expert review, or expensive simulation rather than inexpensive model inference. Test input prioritization addresses this problem by ranking inputs so that a limited verification budget reveals as many regression faults as possible. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single-model confidence scores and do not exploit how predictions, decision boundaries, and local neighborhoods change between model versions. We propose RiskBlend, a classifier-agnostic prioritization framework that combines four complementary risk signals: historical failure patterns, prediction shift, decision-boundary shift, and neighborhood change. These signals are combined using validation-learned APFD-squared weighting. Across four datasets, five classifiers, four regression-update scenarios, and 15 random seeds, totaling 1,200 experimental configurations, RiskBlend achieves the highest average APFD in all 80 dataset-classifier-scenario combinations, with improvements of up to 0.32 APFD over the strongest baseline. Confidence-based methods remain competitive primarily for linear classifiers on sparse categorical features, which we attribute to feature-space geometry. The results show that cross-version behavioral signals provide important complementary information for prioritizing regression faults in machine learning systems.

</details>


### [28] [DART-FL: Burst-Aware Multitask Federated Learning under Dynamic Inference Demand at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27713)
*Yiming Xie,Pinrui Yu,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DART-FL，一个SLO感知、需求驱动的多任务联邦学习框架，在资源受限的边缘设备上联合调整推理与训练的资源分配以及任务级训练权重，以满足推理服务级别目标（SLO）并优先训练高需求任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统需要模型训练与在线推理在资源受限设备上共存，但推理需求随时间变化显著。这带来两个耦合挑战：(1)必须保留足够的计算资源用于推理以满足SLO；(2)剩余训练能力应适应任务特定需求，使高频请求任务能更早地在训练中提升。现有方法未同时解决这两个问题。

Method: DART-FL框架包含三个关键组件：(1)基于推理积压队列和已配置服务容量，确定每个调度周期内推理所需的最小资源分配；(2)将剩余训练容量通过队列感知的DPP启发式调度器在任务间分配；(3)将任务分配映射为动态损失权重，使高推理需求任务在早期通信轮次中获得更大训练权重。客户端训练共享主干网络和任务特定头，完整多任务模型通过FedAvg聚合。

Result: 在Stanford Cars和Oxford Flowers 102数据集上，使用合成和真实阿里云trace驱动的工作负载进行实验评估。结果表明DART-FL能动态调整推理-训练资源划分以适应时变的推理需求，并将高需求任务的学习进度向其突发请求时段偏移，在任务频繁请求时提升模型准确率，同时保持可比的长期多任务性能。

Conclusion: DART-FL有效解决边缘场景下推理与训练的资源竞争问题，通过需求驱动的任务调度和动态损失权重，在不牺牲长期多任务性能的前提下优先提升高需求任务在关键时段的准确性。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems increasingly require model training and online inference to coexist on resource-constrained devices, while inference demand can vary substantially across tasks over time. This creates two coupled challenges: sufficient computation must be reserved for inference to maintain service-level objectives (SLOs), while the remaining training capacity should adapt to task-specific demand so that frequently requested tasks can improve earlier during training.
  We propose an SLO-aware, demand-driven multitask federated learning framework (DART-FL) that jointly adapts the inference-training resource split and task-level training emphasis. At each scheduling interval, DART-FL uses the inference backlog and profiled service capacity to determine the minimum resource allocation required for inference. The remaining training capacity is then distributed across tasks using a queue-aware DPP-inspired scheduler, and the resulting task allocations are mapped to dynamic loss weights. This allows tasks experiencing higher inference demand to receive greater training emphasis in earlier communication rounds. Clients train a shared backbone with task-specific heads, and the complete multitask model is aggregated through FedAvg.
  We evaluate DART-FL using Stanford Cars and Oxford Flowers 102 under both synthetic and real Alibaba trace-derived workloads. Results show that DART-FL dynamically adapts the inference-training resource split to time-varying inference demand and shifts the learning progress of high-demand tasks toward their burst periods, improving model accuracy when those tasks are frequently requested while maintaining comparable long-term multitask performance.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Non-IID: Learner--Client Distribution Mismatch in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27715)
*Yiming Xie,Lili Su,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于代理数据集的影响力感知动态客户端选择框架，用于解决联邦学习中学习者目标分布与异构客户端数据分布不一致的问题，通过在CIFAR-10上的实验验证其收敛速度和精度均优于静态和动态基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统默认聚合的客户端数据分布能代表学习者的目标分布，但该假设在现实中往往不成立；传统客户端选择策略忽略了这种分布错位，而多源迁移学习的工作要么需要直接访问本地数据，要么使用一次性模型/特征聚合，无法有效处理动态、异构场景下的客户端选择问题。

Method: 提出一个动态的、影响力感知的客户端选择框架。学习者维护一个小型代理数据集，通过代理影响力信号估计每个客户端对学习者优化目标的潜在效用，利用留一法（leave-one-out）评估来优先选择最具信息量的知识来源，同时控制统计噪声和数据异构性的负面影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上、异构数据划分条件下进行实验，结果表明所提出的方法在收敛速度和最终精度上一致优于静态和动态基线方法，验证了客户端贡献在不同训练轮次间存在显著差异，且传统方法难以有效识别有益来源。

Conclusion: 通过动态、影响力感知的客户端选择能够有效缓解学习者与客户端群体之间的分布错位问题，为联邦学习在真实异构场景下的部署提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Federated learning systems are increasingly deployed to facilitate collaborative model training across a heterogeneous client population. Existing practice mostly implicitly assumes that the aggregated client data distribution is representative of the learner's target distribution or that learning from all available clients is uniformly beneficial for the learner distribution. However, such an assumption often does not hold in reality. Traditional client selection strategies in FL literature largely overlook such misalignment, while most existing work on multi-source transfer learning either requires direct access to local data or uses one-shot model/feature aggregation.
  In this paper, we take the initiative to understand and mitigate the impacts of such learner-client population misalignment. In particular, we consider the practical setting where the learner keeps a small proxy dataset. We observe that client contributions vary significantly across training rounds, and traditional technology is insufficient to identify beneficial sources under multi-source transfer diversity. Then, we propose a dynamic, influence-aware client selection framework that estimates each client's potential utility to the learner's optimization objective using proxy influence signals on a learner-specific proxy set. Via using leave-one-out evaluations, we prioritize the most informative sources of knowledge while controlling the negative impacts of statistical noise and data heterogeneity. Experiments on CIFAR-10 under heterogeneous data partitions demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms static and dynamic baselines, achieving faster convergence and higher accuracy.

</details>


### [30] [Leveraging a Foundation Model for the EEG-Based Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27719)
*Maggie Lin,Chung-Lin Hou,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LaBraM大语言模型与随机森林分类器结合的AD诊断框架，利用8秒EEG片段实现89.36% ROC-AUC的分类性能，优于传统频谱方法，并通过事后分析验证了所捕获的神经生理学标志物与临床指标的显著相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病存在显著的生物学异质性，传统线性分析方法无法捕捉EEG中的非线性神经动态，导致诊断精度受限。需要一种能够从高维、噪声EEG信号中提取鲁棒疾病标志物的新方法。

Method: 采用在2500+小时EEG数据上预训练的LaBraM模型提取高维潜在嵌入特征，结合非线性的Random Forest分类器进行痴呆与健康对照的判别。在严格的受试者独立5折交叉验证下评估，并通过事后遮挡分析与神经生理对齐分析验证模型捕获的临床生物标志物。

Result: 模型在仅使用8秒EEG片段的情况下，实现ROC-AUC 89.36%±3.49%、PR AUC 81.45%±4.43%、平衡准确率82.44%±4.34%，优于传统带功率与FOOOF参数化振荡特征。遮挡分析显示模型捕获枕-额叶Alpha与Theta节律退化；高痴呆概率预测与较差的认知表现、更高的临床严重程度、增加的theta/alpha相对功率及更高的无周期指数显著相关。

Conclusion: 深度潜在表征能够从噪声EEG信号中提取临床相关的疾病特征，实现精确、快速且数据高效的AD诊断，为临床决策支持提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Biological heterogeneity in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) poses a critical diagnostic challenge, particularly for traditional linear methods that fail to capture non-linear neural dynamics. To address this, we propose a diagnostic framework utilizing the Large Brain Model (LaBraM), pretrained on over 2,500 hours of EEG data. By integrating these high-dimensional latent embeddings with a non-linear Random Forest classifier, our approach effectively isolates robust disease markers. Under a rigorous subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation protocol, the method achieves an ROC-AUC of 89.36% +/- 3.49%, PR AUC of 81.45% +/- 4.43%, and Balanced Accuracy of 82.44% +/- 4.34% in distinguishing dementia patients from healthy controls. Notably, this performance uses only 8-second EEG segments, surpassing traditional spectral baselines, including band-power and parameterized oscillatory features (FOOOF). Post-hoc occlusion analysis confirms the model captures clinically validated biomarkers, specifically occipital-frontal Alpha and Theta rhythm degradation. Additional neurophysiological alignment analysis demonstrated that higher LaBraM-predicted dementia probability significantly correlated with worse cognitive performance, greater clinical severity, increased theta and alpha relative power, and higher aperiodic exponent. These findings demonstrate that deep latent representations extract clinically relevant signatures from noisy signals, enabling precise, rapid, and data-efficient diagnosis.

</details>


### [31] [Diffusion Distillation for Efficient Weather Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27728)
*Yiming Yang,Valentin Brekke,James Briant,Serge Guillas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于能量距离的监督蒸馏方法，将多步扩散教师模型压缩为单步学生模型，在全局预报和台风路径预测中超越现有蒸馏方法，单步推理即可匹配或超越教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能够生成高质量的天气集合预报，但需要昂贵的迭代采样过程，计算成本高，限制了其实际应用效率。

Method: 提出监督能量距离蒸馏方法，通过对齐学生预测与教师样本及地面真实观测，将多步扩散教师压缩为单步学生模型。

Result: 在全局预报和台风路径预测任务上，学生模型超越现有蒸馏方法，在极端事件上保持技能，仅用每次自回归一步神经函数评估即可匹配或超越教师模型性能。

Conclusion: 该蒸馏方法在保持扩散模型集合预报质量的同时显著提升了推理效率，为天气集合预报的实际部署提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate skillful weather ensembles but require costly iterative sampling. We introduce a supervised energy-distance distillation method that compresses a multi-step diffusion teacher into a single-step student by aligning student forecasts with teacher samples and ground-truth observations. Experiments on global forecasting and typhoon-track prediction show that our student outperforms existing distillation methods and preserves skill for extreme events. It matches or surpasses the teacher across key metrics using only one neural function evaluation per autoregressive step.

</details>


### [32] [The Calls are Coming from Inside the Model: Investigating Probe-based Detection of Tool-Calling Errors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27750)
*Eric Yeats,Brendan Kennedy,Loc Truong,John Buckheit,Jung Lee,Jesse Friedbaum,John Emanuello,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用线性探针（linear probes）检测LLM工具调用错误的有效性，在Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard上的18个工具调用LLM上进行评估。研究发现探针能有效捕捉多种工具调用错误，包括类型正确但值错误的参数错误（这类错误可能不被标准日志框架记录），且模型规模、探针层位置和模型后训练类型是影响效果的关键因素。探针还能泛化到新型错误，对实际部署具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的隐藏状态包含难以从输入输出中提取的知识和行为信息。随着LLM越来越多地与外部世界交互，检测错误或不恰当的工具使用成为一个关键的安全问题。标准日志框架可能无法记录某些错误（如类型正确但值错误的参数），因此需要更深入的方法来检测工具调用错误。

Method: 使用线性探针（linear probes）从LLM的内部隐藏状态中检测工具调用错误，在Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard上的18个工具调用LLM上进行系统评估，分析探针有效性及影响因素（模型大小、探针层位置、模型后训练类型），并测试探针对新型错误的泛化能力。

Result: 线性探针是检测各类工具调用错误的有效手段，能捕捉包括参数值错误（但类型正确）等标准日志框架可能遗漏的错误类型。探针有效性受模型规模、探针层选择和模型后训练类型影响，且具备对新型错误类型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于隐藏状态的线性探针为检测LLM工具调用错误提供了一种可行且可泛化的方案，对于LLM在实际部署中的安全性和可靠性保障具有重要意义。未来可进一步优化探针的层选择策略和跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: The hidden states of large language models (LLMs) are known to capture rich information relating to model knowledge and behavior that can be hard to extract from examination of input and output alone. As LLM-based systems increasingly interface with the external world, one area of concern is detecting incorrect or improper use of tools. Motivated by this, we study the effectiveness of using linear probes to detect incorrect tool-calls, measuring probe efficacy across 18 tool-calling LLMs evaluated on the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard. Overall, we find that probing is an effective means to catch a range of different tool-calling errors, including errors arising from using an argument that has the wrong value but the correct type, which might not be recorded by standard logging frameworks. Important factors in success include model size, probing layer, and model post-training type. We also show that probes are capable of generalizing to novel types of errors, which is critical in real world deployments.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Search-Imitation: Prior-Directed Exploration for Searchless Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27757)
*Szymon Miłosz,Piotr Duch,Szymon Grabowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无搜索（searchless）国际象棋网络。针对从教师（Lc0 Chessformer）模仿MCTS访问计数训练出的单次前向传播网络，作者采用自对弈强化学习微调，用前向KL到自身MCTS先验的探索（prior-directed exploration）替代传统的反向KL熵正则，结合基于价值头不确定性的自适应采样温度。约两千步训练将谜题准确率从93.9%提升至94.9%，四步杀准确率从77%升至81%，同时保持无搜索强度。进一步发现战术准确率与棋力可分离：仅用谜题微调的对照模型获得最大战术提升却损失约260 Elo。前向KL先验在评级榜上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿搜索（MCTS访问计数）是玩无搜索棋的差代理，直接模仿教师生成的策略并未针对单次前向传播的棋力进行优化，因此需要自对弈强化学习微调来提升无搜索强度。

Method: 采用自对弈RL微调；以网络自身MCTS先验替代均匀分布作为探索目标，使用前向（mass-covering）KL散度引导探索覆盖先验认为有希望的着法；配合熵自适应采样温度（由价值头不确定性决定），在局面已定时锐化采样分布。

Result: 约两千步训练将100,000谜题套件准确率从93.9%提高至94.9%，四步杀准确率从77%提高至81%，无搜索强度保持或略高于基线。发现战术准确率与棋力解耦：战术增益集中在一个百分点带内而评级围绕基线波动；仅谜题微调对照损失约260 Elo却获最大战术增益。前向KL先验在评级榜居首，并与反向KL锚统计并列。

Conclusion: 前向KL到MCTS先验的探索是对无搜索围棋网络微调的有效策略，能在保持棋力下提升战术准确性。战术能力与全局棋力并不等价，更好的解谜者不一定更强的棋手。分布级测量表明先验锚定可防止自对弈崩溃到单一对局线，且新解的谜题恰是那些先验保留致胜着法的边缘案例。

Abstract: Searchless chess networks reach human master strength from a single forward pass by imitating a stronger teacher: the strongest, Leela Chess Zero's (Lc0) released Chessformer, distills the visit counts of an AlphaZero-style Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). Imitating a search is a poor proxy for playing without one, so we fine-tune for single-pass strength with self-play reinforcement learning (RL).
