</details>


### [82] [Image Augmentation as Test Generation for Deep Learning-Based Image Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27502)
*Yehan De Silva,Anirudh Sridhar,Armin Lotfy,Nafiseh Kahani,Yvan Labiche,Ziyu Wang,Frank Ouyang,Clare Carty,Azalia Shamsaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了双重贡献：一是对图像增强/生成技术进行了文献综述，识别出50种技术并归类为十大类别的分类体系；二是大规模实证研究，评估这些技术作为嵌入式图像检索系统测试生成器的有效性。实验基于Amazon Titan和OpenCLIP嵌入模型、三个数据集（CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K和工业数据集March Networks），从嵌入空间相似性、不确定性和语义真实性等维度评估，发现天气模拟和SaSPA技术最能产生高嵌入不确定性和失败率，同时保持性能稳定和视觉真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习图像检索系统需要可靠的测试方法，但缺乏系统性的测试输入生成技术与实证评估，特别是如何利用图像增强/生成技术构建有效测试集并减少人工标注成本是亟待解决的软件工程难题。

Method: 首先进行文献综述，归纳50种增强/生成技术为十类别分类体系；然后在大规模实证研究中，使用Amazon Titan和OpenCLIP作为嵌入模型，从嵌入空间相似性、四种不确定估计器、LLaVA语义真实性评分和检索失败率四个维度评估这些技术，在三个数据集上进行实验。

Result: 天气模拟和SaSPA技术产生最高的嵌入不确定性和失败率，同时保持性能稳定性、视觉真实感和增强有效性的良好平衡；GAN类增强技术在真实性方面排名最低，存在合成伪影和感知不一致问题；结果具有配置特异性。

Conclusion: 研究为图像检索系统提供了增强/生成技术选择的实用指南，强调最大化测试多样性同时保持真实图像特征的重要性，并通过元变形测试降低人工数据标注成本，支持构建全面有效的测试套件。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of deep learning-based image retrieval systems is a software engineering challenge. This paper presents a dual contribution: (1) a literature review of augmentation and generation techniques which resulted in the identification of 50 techniques which we organized into a ten-category taxonomy, and (2) a large-scale empirical study that evaluates these techniques as test generators for embedding-based image retrieval systems. Augmented images are embedded using Amazon Titan and OpenCLIP, and evaluated across four analytical dimensions: (1) embedding-space similarity, (2) embedding uncertainty measured via four estimators, (3) semantic realism scored by LLaVA, and (4) retrieval failure rate. Experiments are performed on three datasets: CIFAR-10, ImageNet-1K, and a dataset from an industrial partner (March Networks). Across all evaluated datasets and embedding models, and under the single severity level tested for each technique, weather simulation and SaSPA are the image augmentation/generation techniques that produce the highest embedding uncertainty and failure rates while maintaining a favorable balance between performance stability, visual realism, and augmentation effectiveness. The results we discuss are configuration-specific and may shift under milder or stronger perturbation settings. In contrast, GAN-based augmentation techniques are among the lowest in realism, indicating the presence of synthetic artifacts and perceptual inconsistencies that reduce their suitability to produce realistic test inputs. Overall, our findings provide practical guidelines for selecting augmentation techniques that maximize test diversity while preserving realistic image characteristics, thereby enabling the construction of comprehensive and effective test suites for image retrieval systems while reducing the cost of manual data labeling through the use of metamorphic testing.

</details>


### [83] [Predicting LLM Performance from Prompt Linguistic Features: An Empirical Study in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27621)
*Quim Motger,Alessio Miaschi,Xavier Franch,Mohammad Amin Zadenoori,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了提示词（prompt）的语言学属性能否在推理前预测LLM性能，通过生成9,000个受语言学控制的提示词变体并训练回归模型，发现语言学特征可显著预测LLM在二分类需求任务上的性能（R²∈[0.38,0.42]），其中句法与形态句法特征贡献最大，为低成本提示词筛选与优化提供了可解释的先验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对提示词措辞高度敏感，微小改动即可显著影响输出质量，但实践者缺乏在推理前评估提示词的原则性方法，提示词选择只能依赖昂贵的LLM调用和试错。作者旨在利用可测量的语言学属性在推理前低成本预测LLM性能。

Method: 从100个初始提示词出发，通过变化30个语言学指标生成9,000个语言学受控的提示词变体，使用五个开源LLM在625条标注需求上进行评估；采用分层10折交叉验证训练回归预测器，结合置换检验进行显著性测试，并通过特征重要性分析识别跨LLM与模型特定的预测因子。

Result: 语言学特征在各项指标上均显著预测提示词性能（R²∈[0.38,0.42]，q<0.05）。句法和形态句法特征驱动了大部分预测信号；跨LLM的预测因子包括复合依赖分布、连接词密度以及词/句长度，反映了对领域词汇和复杂结构的敏感性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，降低LLM性能的语言模式与增加人类理解难度的语言模式存在重叠，且词汇多样性作为质量维度并不相关。语言学画像结合标准回归可提供有效、可解释、低成本的先验，可用于昂贵优化流程之前的提示词筛选与改进。

Abstract: Background. LLM outputs are highly sensitive to prompt formulation: small wording changes can substantially affect output quality. This matters in software engineering, where prompts guide requirements analysis, code generation, and artefact synthesis. Poor formulations yield unreliable artefacts, yet practitioners lack principled ways to assess a prompt before inference, making selection depend on costly LLM calls and trial-and-error refinement. Aims. We investigate whether measurable linguistic properties of prompts can predict LLM performance before inference, enabling low-cost prompt selection and refinement, validated on binary requirements classification targeting F1, F2, precision, and recall. Method. We generate 9,000 linguistically controlled prompt variants from 100 initial prompts by varying 30 linguistic metrics, evaluated with five open-source LLMs on 625 annotated requirements. Regression predictors are trained via stratified 10-fold cross-validation with permutation-based significance testing; feature importance analysis identifies cross-LLM and model-specific predictors. Results. Linguistic features significantly predict prompt performance across all targets (R2 in [0.38,0.42], q<0.05). Syntactic and morphosyntactic features drive most predictive signal; cross-LLM predictors include compound dependency distribution, conjunction density, and word/sentence length, reflecting sensitivity to domain vocabulary and complex structures. Conclusions. Results suggest practical implications for prompt engineering, including overlap between linguistic patterns that reduce LLM performance and those that increase human comprehension difficulty, and the irrelevance of lexical variety as a quality dimension. More broadly, linguistic profiling combined with standard regression provides an effective, interpretable, low-cost prior before costly optimisation pipelines.

</details>


### [84] [Operationalizing Regulations into Code: A Model to Enhance Governance and Compliance in LLM Selection for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27703)
*Jonysberg Quintino,Hermano Moura,Filipe Calegário*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三层治理与合规模型，用于在软件工程项目中支持LLM选型的监管合规决策，并通过DSR方法开发和试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM集成到SDLC虽能提升开发者生产力，但模型选型阶段引入了安全、隐私和合规风险。欧盟AI法案、NIST AI RMF、GDPR、LGPD、ISO/IEC 42001等法规和框架设定的义务难以转化为技术决策的可操作标准，迫切需要填补这一鸿沟。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（DSR）方法构建三层模型：（i）监管需求层；（ii）组织治理能力层，由多准则决策矩阵实例化，包含一票否决（K.O.）和加权评分准则；（iii）生产力与可持续性结果层，由LLM治理评估协议（PAG-LLM）操作化。监管反馈回路将运营结果连接回规范性层，支持模型迭代改进。试点评估使用基于CWE和OWASP Top 10的20个对抗性场景。

Result: 试点实验表明，商业云端LLM与本地开源LLM呈现不同的风险画像。监管性否决逻辑（尤其是K.O.准则）能够阻止选择技术上具备竞争力但存在不可接受的合规风险的模型，证明了在软件工程项目中进行治理导向的LLM选型的可行性。

Conclusion: 该模型为LLM选型中的监管合规义务向技术决策标准转化提供了可操作的框架，初步证据支持其可行性，为软件工程中治理导向的LLM选型提供了新路径。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC) can improve developer productivity, but it also introduces security, privacy, and compliance risks during model selection. Regulations and frameworks such as the EU AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework (RMF), the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), the Lei Geral de Proteção de Dados (LGPD), and ISO/IEC 42001 establish obligations that are often difficult to translate into operational criteria for technical decision-making. This paper proposes a model to support governance and compliance in LLM selection for software engineering projects. The model is developed through Design Science Research (DSR) and is structured in three layers: (i) regulatory requirements, (ii) organizational governance capabilities, instantiated by a multi-criteria decision matrix with knock-out and weighted scoring criteria, and (iii) productivity and sustainability outcomes, operationalized by the LLM governance assessment protocol (PAG-LLM). A regulatory feedback loop connects operational results back to the normative layer, enabling iterative refinement of the model. A pilot evaluation with 20 adversarial scenarios based on Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) and the OWASP Top 10 suggests distinct risk profiles between commercial cloud-based LLMs and local open-source LLMs. The results provide preliminary evidence that regulatory disqualification logic, particularly K.O. criteria, can prevent the selection of technically competitive models that nonetheless pose unacceptable compliance risks, demonstrating the feasibility of governance-oriented LLM selection in software engineering projects.

</details>


### [85] [Decoupling is a Necessity: Transformation-Agnostic Decompiled Code Recovery under Optimization and Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27889)
*Zhiping Zhou,Xiaohong Li,Ruitao Feng,Yao Zhang,Yuekang Li,Wenbu Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReSource是首个面向转换无关源代码恢复的多阶段LLM框架，通过将二进制到源代码的差异解耦为词法、句法和语义三个正交层次，并引入语义扭曲数据库、轻量级结构预测器和上下文词法推演，在超过80,000个反编译-源代码函数对上实现了83%的Top-5源检索准确率和0.66的平均相似度，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的反编译工具在生产二进制环境中（编译器优化与对抗性混淆共同作用）经常遭受结构坍塌和语义幻觉，导致高保真源代码恢复效果不佳。

Method: ReSource将二进制-源代码差异分为词法、句法和语义三个正交层级，并相应解耦恢复流程：(1)从语义扭曲数据库中检索经验先验以约束LLM防止逻辑漂移；(2)集成轻量级预测器重建源代码级结构骨架以解决控制流平坦化问题；(3)通过上下文词法推演阶段优化标识符恢复可读性。

Result: 在涵盖三种优化级别和四种混淆技术的80,000余个函数对的基准上，ReSource达到83%的Top-5源检索准确率和0.66的平均相似度，在语义可辨识性方面优于DeGPT、LLM4Decompile和FidelityGPT等基线。

Conclusion: ReSource为下游安全分析提供了可扩展、可靠的源代码恢复基础，在保持鲁棒语义可辨识性的同时克服了现有LLM反编译工具的过拟合和退化问题。

Abstract: Reverse engineering is essential for software security analysis and vulnerability detection. Decompilation, the process of lifting binaries to high-level pseudocode, is central to this task. However, production binaries are hostile environments: aggressive compiler optimizations and adversarial obfuscation jointly mangle control structures, obscure variable intents, and disguise high-level program logic. Consequently, existing LLM-based decompilation tools frequently suffer from structural collapse and semantic hallucinations. We present ReSource, the first multi-phase LLM framework designed for transformation-agnostic source recovery. To tackle these intertwined distortions, ReSource conceptualizes the binary-to-source discrepancies into three orthogonal tiers, namely lexical, syntactic, and semantic, and decouples the recovery process accordingly. First, to ground the LLM and prevent logic drift, it retrieves empirical priors from a curated Semantic Distortion Database. Second, to resolve control-flow flattening, it integrates a lightweight predictor to reconstruct the source-level structural skeleton. Finally, a contextual lexical deduction stage refines identifiers to restore human readability. Evaluated on a massive benchmark of over 80,000 decompiled-source function pairs across three optimization levels and four obfuscation techniques, ReSource achieves an 83% Top-5 source retrieval accuracy and an average similarity score of 0.66. By maintaining robust semantic identifiability where state-of-the-art baselines (DeGPT, LLM4Decompile, and FidelityGPT) severely overfit or degrade, ReSource provides a scalable and reliable foundation for downstream security analysis.

</details>


### [86] [Antipatterns in AI-assisted Qualitative Data Analysis: A Catalog of Temptations and Pitfalls for Software Engineering Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27927)
*Rashina Hoda,Carolyn Seaman,Victoria Gomes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于作者多年的定性软件工程研究经验，提出了一份AI辅助定性数据分析（QDA）中的反模式目录，将其分为三类（危险驱动、操作失误、分析失败），旨在帮助SE研究者识别和避免常见陷阱，同时为审稿人提供评判准则，推动负责任的人机协作定性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助定性数据分析为软件工程研究提供了前所未有的效率提升机会，但不加批判地使用会损害分析严谨性并导致低质量研究的泛滥。目前缺乏战略性指导来帮助SE研究者识别和规避AI辅助QDA中的方法论风险。

Method: 基于作者在定性SE研究方面的数十年专业经验和知识，结合对AI辅助QDA新兴领域的理解，提出并编纂了一份反模式目录。反模式被定义为"表面上看起来有利、但最终削弱分析严谨性和有效性"的假设与实践，并按影响程度分为三个递进类别：危险驱动（Dangerous Drivers）、操作失误（Operational Missteps）和分析失败（Analytical Failures）。

Result: 产出了一份系统性的AI辅助QDA反模式目录，包含三大类别，既可作为研究者的避坑指南，也可为审稿人提供识别和批评有缺陷实践的词汇与评判标准。

Conclusion: 这份反模式目录可作为软件工程领域定性研究方法负责任演进的基础，迈向有原则、有意义的人机协作定性研究。

Abstract: AI-assisted qualitative data analysis (QDA) offers unprecedented opportunities to streamline software engineering (SE) research, yet uncritical use risks compromising analytical rigor and flooding the field with accelerated production of low-quality research. While tactical best practices will naturally evolve over time, SE researchers currently lack strategic guidance to identify and mitigate methodological risks when attempting AI-assisted QDA. Based on our decades of qualitative SE research expertise and experience combined with an understanding of the emerging landscape of AI-assisted QDA, this paper presents a catalog of antipatterns in AI-assisted QDA - a set of assumptions and practices that initially appear advantageous but ultimately undermine analytical rigor and validity. The antipatterns are grouped into three categories reflecting escalating impact: Dangerous Drivers, Operational Missteps, and Analytical Failures. As more SE researchers attempt AI-assisted QDA, these antipatterns will help them identify and avoid common temptations and pitfalls, while reviewers can be equipped with the vocabulary and criteria to call out problematic and failed practice. Ultimately, this catalog of antipatterns can serve as a stepping stone in our responsible methodological evolution toward principled and meaningful human-AI collaboration in qualitative research.

</details>


### [87] [CC4M: Code Clone Analysis and Visualization for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28111)
*Gen Kawamata,Yuki Ota,Norihiro Yoshida,Shiyu Yang,Erina Makihara,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CC4M是一个微服务感知的克隆分析可视化工具，能够检测并丰富跨服务边界的代码克隆信息，并提供交互式散点图帮助开发者识别潜在的高维护影响克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构旨在通过将系统分解为小型、松散耦合的服务来实现独立部署和演化，但已有研究表明代码克隆存在于服务边界之间，其中一些克隆会在同一版本中被共同修改。这种跨服务边界的克隆需要同步变更，削弱了服务独立性并增加维护成本。然而，现有工具不支持微服务感知的克隆分析。

Method: CC4M检测克隆对，并为其补充服务边界、共同修改、文件类别和度量信息。丰富后的克隆在带有明确服务边界的交互式散点图中可视化，支持基于度量的过滤以优先处理可能具有更高维护影响的克隆。

Result: 使用一个开源微服务应用程序进行演示，展示了CC4M如何帮助识别代码变更的潜在影响范围。工具和演示视频已公开提供。

Conclusion: CC4M填补了现有工具在微服务感知克隆分析方面的空白，通过服务边界与共修改等维度丰富克隆信息并可视化，帮助开发者在微服务环境中更有效地管理跨服务克隆和维护成本。

Abstract: Microservice architecture supports software evolution by decomposing a system into small, loosely coupled services that can be deployed independently. Contrary to the expectation of high modularity, prior studies have reported that code clones exist across service boundaries, some of which are co-modified in the same version. Such clones may require changes to be propagated across service boundaries, thereby undermining service independence and increasing maintenance costs. However, existing tools do not support microservice-aware clone analysis. We present CC4M, a microservice-aware clone analysis and visualization tool. CC4M detects and enriches clone pairs with service-boundary, co-modification, file-category, and metric information. The enriched clones are visualized in an interactive scatter plot with explicit service boundaries, supporting metric-based filtering to prioritize clones with potentially higher maintenance impact. Using an open-source microservice application, we illustrate how CC4M helps identify the potential impact scope of code changes. A demo video and the tool are available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0xOIQPFbkUg and https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21204195, respectively.

</details>


### [88] [RESTCov: A Tool for Structural Coverage Analysis of REST APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28114)
*Tolgahan Bardakci,Serge Demeyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RESTCov是一个轻量级工具，基于OpenAPI规范和HTTP请求/响应日志计算REST API的结构覆盖率，覆盖路径、操作、参数、媒体类型、状态码和状态类别，无需访问实现代码即可生成机器可读结果和HTML报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST API广泛用于现代软件系统，但开发者和测试者难以了解测试套件覆盖了API规范的哪些部分。传统的覆盖率分析依赖源代码插桩，而这对于分布式、外部维护、仅可通过黑盒执行访问的REST API来说不切实际。

Method: RESTCov从OpenAPI规范和观测到的HTTP请求/响应日志中计算结构化的REST API覆盖率，涵盖路径、操作、参数、媒体类型、状态码和状态类别六个维度。工具同时输出机器可读结果和人类可读的HTML报告，帮助用户检查覆盖缺口、诊断规范与日志的不匹配，并评估测试套件质量。

Result: RESTCov能够在无需访问实现的前提下，对REST API测试套件进行多维度的结构覆盖率分析，并提供可视化的HTML报告以辅助理解覆盖情况和发现规范-日志不一致问题。

Conclusion: RESTCov提供了一种轻量级的黑盒REST API覆盖率分析方法，弥补了传统源代码插桩方法在分布式、外部维护API场景下的不足，有助于改进REST API的测试评估与质量保障。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used in modern software systems, but developers and testers often lack visibility into which parts of an API specification are exercised by a test suite. Traditional coverage analysis usually relies on source-code instrumentation, which is impractical for REST APIs that are distributed, externally maintained, and hence accessible only through black-box execution. This paper presents RESTCov, a lightweight tool that computes structural REST API coverage from an OpenAPI specification and observed HTTP request/response logs, reporting coverage across paths, operations, parameters, media types, status codes, and status classes. RESTCov produces both machine-readable results and a human-readable HTML report, helping users inspect coverage gaps, diagnose specification-log mismatches, and evaluate REST API test suites without requiring access to the implementation.
  Screencast: https://youtu.be/mNz2P43OyUc
  Repository: https://github.com/2tolgahan2/RESTCov

</details>


### [89] [Post-Edit Re-Verification in Simulator-Backed Engineering Agents: A Controlled Comparison of Verification-Cadence Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28147)
*Qingchuan Zhu,Shuyue Tong,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究考察在修改后重新验证过程中，明确的验证节奏指导（显式指示在重大修改后请求新仿真）是否影响agent的重新验证行为。基于DWSIM仿真器和连续阀门压力调整任务，对5个阿里/Qwen模型各执行120次评估槽位，结果显示保留验证节奏指导（CG）比省略（CO）产生更多重新验证（94 vs 32）、更少节奏违规（26 vs 87）和更高的有界最终成功率（95 vs 35），且qwen3.5-35b-a3b在两种条件下幾乎都不重新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与外部仿真器交互的agent在修改设计后需要重新获取针对修改后状态的工程证据，但现有研究未明确考察显式的验证节奏指导（即是否告知agent在实质性修改后请求新仿真）对首次编辑后重新验证行为的影响。作者希望通过分离"指令条件性策略遵从"与"自发识别证据过时"来澄清这一因素的作用。

Method: 研究采用对照实验设计，两种条件分别为Cadence-Guided（CG，保留"重大修改后请求新仿真"的明确指令）和Cadence-Omitted（CO，移除该指令），两者均不设硬性门控。使用DWSIM作为仿真后端，任务为连续阀门压力调整，评估5个Alibaba/Qwen模型在8个合成案例上的表现，每个模型-案例-条件组合执行3次实时API调用，每种条件共120个评估槽位。

Result: CG条件下观察到94/120次重新验证，CO仅32/120；节奏违规在CG为26/120，CO为87/120；有界最终成功CG为95/120，CO为35/120。qwen3.5-35b-a3b表现异常，在CG和CO中重新验证分别仅为1/24和0/24，且两种条件下均无最终成功。

Conclusion: 在有界协议范围内，明确的编辑后验证节奏指导与更多重新验证、更少节奏违规和更高有界最终成功率相关，支持将验证节奏视为显式交互协议组成部分的观点。同时提示个别模型对该指导的行为响应可能存在显著差异。

Abstract: Engineering agents that interact with external simulators may need to coordinate design modification with reacquisition of engineering evidence for the modified state. We ask whether first post-edit re-verification changes when explicit verification-cadence guidance is retained versus omitted while verification-relevant state/facts are held constant. Cadence-Guided (CG) retained an instruction to request a new simulation after a substantive modification, whereas Cadence-Omitted (CO) removed that instruction; neither condition used a hard gate. The study therefore measures instruction-conditioned post-edit verification-policy adherence rather than spontaneous recognition that prior evidence has become stale. Using DWSIM as the simulator backend and continuous valve-pressure adjustment, five Alibaba/Qwen models were evaluated on eight synthetic cases; each model-case-condition combination was executed three times via live API calls, yielding 120 evaluation slots per condition. Re-verification was observed in 94/120 CG slots versus 32/120 CO slots; cadence violations occurred in 26/120 versus 87/120; and bounded final success was reached in 95/120 versus 35/120. qwen3.5-35b-a3b showed minimal re-verification (1/24 in CG and 0/24 in CO) and no final success in either condition. Within this bounded protocol, explicit post-edit verification-cadence guidance was associated with more re-verification, fewer cadence violations, and more frequent bounded final success, supporting the treatment of verification cadence as an explicit interaction-protocol component.

</details>


### [90] [From Architecture to Binary: Ensuring Cross-Domain Consistency in Model-Based Airborne Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28156)
*Nils Schlautmann,Viktor Sinitsyn,Benjamin Engelhard,Florian Holzapfel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向有人/无人飞行器的机载软件开发方法，通过以仓库为中心的实现（每个领域分配主仓库、跨仓库引用和专用CI流水线）来减少系统域、基于模型的功能域和嵌入式软件域之间的一致性不一致问题，适用于资源受限的小型团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ARP-4754B和DO-178C等标准影响下的航空环境中，跨系统域、模型功能域和嵌入式软件域之间存在不一致问题，这些不一致主要源于领域边界上约束执行不足，而非流程定义缺失。此前提出的基于关系接口数据库的工具链存在重复性失败模式，需要一种更有效的实施方案。

Method: 提出以仓库为中心的架构：每个领域分配一个主仓库，通过跨仓库引用连接，并配备专门的CI流水线用于生成、更新和验证交换工件。该方法包含自动化接口更新、差异变更通知和一致性检查机制，使变更传播所需的人工工作最小化。面向小型、资源受限团队设计，避免了重型流程开销。

Result: 在某进行中的实验项目中完成了初步实现，并获得了早期使用阶段的定性反馈。

Conclusion: 以仓库为中心的接口一致性管理方法能有效在耗时且昂贵的代码生成和编译步骤之前暴露不一致问题，适合在无重型流程负担的小型团队环境中实施。

Abstract: This paper presents an airborne software development approach for manned and unmanned aerial vehicles aimed at reducing inconsistencies across system, model-based functional, and embedded software domains. In environments influenced by standards such as ARP-4754B and DO-178C, these inconsistencies typically stem from insufficient enforcement across domain boundaries rather than missing process definitions. Building on a previously proposed toolchain centered on a relational interface database, we identify recurring failure modes and propose a repository-centered implementation to address them, tailored to small, resource-constrained teams operating without heavyweight process overhead. Each domain is assigned a primary repository with cross-repository references and dedicated CI pipelines that generate, update, and validate the exchanged artifacts. Automated interface updates, differential change notifications, and consistency checks propagate changes with minimal manual effort and surface inconsistencies before the time-consuming code-generation and compilation steps. An initial implementation in an ongoing experimental project is described, with qualitative feedback from its early use.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Strategy Generation for Boundary Value Exploration Beyond Numeric Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28230)
*Sabinakhon Akbarova,Felix Dobslaw,Robert Feldt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ABEX是一个基于代理式LLM的框架，用于自动化边界值探索（BVE），通过自适应策略生成取代手工设计的变异算子，在数值和非数值（字符串、数组、混合）输入上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化BVE技术依赖为每种输入类型甚至每个被测函数手工设计的变异算子，这限制了其应用范围仅限于数值输入。软件行为在输入区域边界处常发生突变且易出错，但缺乏能够自动发现这些边界的通用方法。

Method: ABEX采用代理式LLM框架，由专门的LLM代理提出、选择和执行边界探索策略，并由执行反馈和质量-多样性（QD）档案引导。策略以自然语言表达，可编码类型级和函数级知识，有效的策略还可存储复用。

Result: 在20个函数（数值、字符串、数组和混合输入）上的黑盒评估中：数值函数上10/11超越SOTA QD方法，平均QD分数高11.7倍；非数值函数上首次实现自动化黑盒BVE并发现领域对齐的边界行为；变异测试中ABEX达86.2%变异分数（对比基线61.9%），杀死的顽固突变体数量是基线的9倍。

Conclusion: ABEX通过自适应策略生成和LLM代理协作，成功将自动化BVE从数值输入扩展到非数值输入，显著提升了边界发现能力和故障揭示能力，消融研究确认自适应策略生成是性能提升的主要驱动力。

Abstract: Software behavior often changes abruptly at boundaries between input regions, and these transitions are known to be fault-prone. Boundary Value Exploration (BVE) automates boundary discovery by searching for pairs of similar inputs that nevertheless trigger different program behaviors. Existing automated BVE techniques rely on mutation operators hand-engineered for each input type, or even for each function under test, which has confined their use to numeric inputs. We present ABEX, an agentic LLM-based framework that replaces operator engineering with adaptive strategy generation: specialized LLM agents propose, select, and execute boundary-exploration strategies, guided by execution feedback and a quality-diversity (QD) archive. Because strategies are expressed in natural language, they can encode both type-level and function-specific knowledge, and effective strategies can even be stored and reused. We evaluate ABEX in a black-box setting on 20 functions with numeric, string, array, and mixed inputs. On numeric functions, ABEX outperforms a state-of-the-art QD method on 10 of 11 functions, with average QD-scores 11.7x higher. On non-numeric functions, addressed here for the first time in automated black-box BVE, ABEX discovers domain-aligned boundary behaviors for all subjects. Mutation testing shows the discovered boundaries are fault-revealing: with equally sized test suites, ABEX reaches an average mutation score of 86.2% versus 61.9% for the QD baseline, and kills nine times as many hard-to-detect stubborn mutants. An ablation study identifies adaptive strategy generation as the primary driver of these gains.

</details>


### [92] [Where Does Balance Break? Boundary Discovery for Game Balance Testing under a Finite Simulation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28364)
*Hiroki Mukai,Yusaku Kato,Norihiro Yoshida,Erina Makihara,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BBExplorer，一种在有限仿真预算下将游戏平衡回归测试形式化为边界发现问题的测试方法，通过多方向候选生成、预算感知两阶段筛选和自适应步长收缩来高效定位平衡与不平衡区域边界，并在两类复杂度不同的游戏上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统普遍存在非确定性执行和大规模行为空间，传统软件测试依赖的可复现执行和稳定正确性标准不再适用。竞争性多人游戏是此类系统的典型代表——平衡性至关重要但难以评估，微小的参数变化就可能导致平衡突然崩溃，而检测这类故障需要在非确定性结果和高维参数空间下进行大量重复仿真。因此，亟需一种在有限预算下有效识别平衡边界的方法。

Method: BBExplorer将游戏平衡回归测试形式化为有限仿真预算下的边界发现问题，核心包含三个组件：(1) 多方向候选生成，在参数空间中从多个方向生成候选输入以覆盖不同区域；(2) 预算感知两阶段筛选，在有限仿真预算约束下分阶段筛选出有希望的候选；(3) 自适应步长收缩，迭代细化边界附近区域的探索精度，逐步逼近平衡与不平衡区域的分界。

Result: 在两个不同复杂度级别的游戏上进行的实验表明，BBExplorer在低维参数设置下表现强劲，在高维设置下仍保持有效性。此外，该方法在不同随机种子和阈值设置下展现出稳定的边界行为，说明其具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明BBExplorer对于实际的平衡回归测试是有效的，并且更广泛地适用于非确定性、预算受限系统中的边界导向测试。

Abstract: Software testing often relies on assumptions such as reproducible executions and stable correctness criteria. However, many modern software systems exhibit non-deterministic executions and large behavior spaces, making exhaustive exploration impractical and single-run judgments unreliable. These characteristics make it difficult to identify where acceptable behavior ends and problematic behavior begins. Competitive multiplayer games represent a challenging instance of such systems, where balance must be maintained so that no single strategy dominates. Even small parameter changes can trigger abrupt balance disruption, yet detecting such failures requires repeated simulations under non-deterministic outcomes and high-dimensional parameter spaces. In this paper, we formulate game balance regression testing as a boundary-discovery problem under a finite simulation budget. The objective is to efficiently identify inputs near the boundary that separates balanced and unbalanced regions. To address this problem, we propose BBExplorer, which combines multi-directional candidate generation, budget-aware two-stage screening, and adaptive step-size shrinkage for boundary refinement. Experimental results on two games with different levels of complexity show that the approach is strong in low-dimensional settings and remains effective in higher-dimensional ones. It also exhibits stable boundary behavior across unseen random seeds and threshold settings. These results indicate that BBExplorer is effective for practical balance regression testing and, more broadly, for boundary-oriented testing in non-deterministic, budget-constrained systems.

</details>


### [93] [Sustainability of Open-Source Machine Learning Robustness Assessment Tools: A Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28396)
*Joshua Owotogbe,Indika Kumara,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel,Damian Tamburri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源鲁棒性评估工具生态系统进行了实证研究，从GitHub系统搜索识别出28个鲁棒性工具仓库，使用软件工程度量分析社区参与度、维护活动和项目持久性。结果显示参与和维护分布不均，截至2026年1月21日仅5个仓库活跃、22个不活跃、1个已归档，强调了需要将鲁棒性工具视为不断演化的软件系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adversarial Robustness Toolbox、Foolbox、Robustness Gym等开源工具广泛支持ML系统的鲁棒性测试与评估，实践者常依赖这些工具选择评估依赖、复现鲁棒性评估和提供AI保证证据，但关于这些工具如何被维护、公众参与程度以及长期可持续性的了解却十分匮乏。

Method: 从先前工作衍生的精选种子集出发，系统搜索GitHub识别出28个鲁棒性工具仓库；通过分析仓库工件（repository artifacts），采用成熟的软件工程度量指标（如社区参与度、维护活动和项目持久性）进行特征化评估。

Result: 参与度和维护活动分布不均衡，持续活跃集中于少数仓库子集。截至数据收集日期2026年1月21日，5个仓库被分类为活跃、22个不活跃、1个已归档。

Conclusion: 研究发现凸显了将鲁棒性工具视为持续演化的软件系统的必要性，而非一次性交付的静态工件，以更好地支持AI系统的鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: Robustness evaluation is essential for deploying machine-learning (ML) systems in real-world settings, where models may face adversarial perturbations, distribution shifts, and other operational stressors. Many open-source tools, including Adversarial Robustness Toolbox, Foolbox, and Robustness Gym, support robustness testing and evaluation. However, little is known about how these tools are maintained, publicly engaged with, and sustained over time, even though practitioners may rely on them to select evaluation dependencies, reproduce robustness assessments, and provide evidence for AI assurance. We present an empirical study of the open-source robustness tooling ecosystem. Starting from a curated seed set derived from prior work, we systematically searched GitHub and identified 28 robustness-tool repositories. We analyzed repository artifacts to characterize observable community engagement, maintenance activity, and project longevity using established software-engineering metrics. Our results show that engagement and maintenance are unevenly distributed, with sustained activity concentrated in a small subset of repositories. At the data collection date of January 21, 2026, five repositories were classified as active, 22 as inactive, and one as archived. These findings highlight the need to treat robustness tools as evolving software systems.

</details>


### [94] [Recovering Software Architecture Intent from Historical Work Items using Generative AI: A Mixed-Methods Industry Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28403)
*Dominik Storck,Tobias Eisenreich,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的半自动五步工作流，从历史敏捷工作项（Azure DevOps）中恢复C4架构图，并通过混合方法（专家访谈+定量稳定性分析）在两个工业项目上验证了其可行性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构往往只被部分地捕获在代码中，大量设计意图散落在不断演进的项目工件（如工作项、用户故事、追踪文档）中。这些工件虽然保留了设计意图的痕迹，却很少能直接支撑架构分析，因此需要一种方法从这些非结构化开发过程工件中恢复架构视图。

Method: 提出一个半自动的五步LLM管道工作流，核心包括：提示链（prompt chain）、双向可追溯性（bidirectional traceability）和思维链推理（Chain-of-Thought reasoning）。该工作流将非结构化的Azure DevOps工作项转换生成C4架构图视觉工件。评估采用混合方法设计，结合定性专家访谈与定量稳定性分析，在两个行业项目上进行验证。

Result: 实践者认为生成的架构基线在系统理解方面准确且高度有用。工件严格受限于输入数据，能反映文档化意图，从而暴露与已实现现实之间的差异和架构漂移。定量分析显示，工作流对架构实体具有高稳定性，但对实体间关系的稳定性较低，且相对方差在生成步骤间累积放大。

Conclusion: 该工作流证明了基于开发过程工件进行LLM辅助架构恢复的实际可行性，为在敏捷项目中利用历史工作项进行架构洞察提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Software architecture is often only partially captured in code, while much of the design intent lives in evolving project artifacts. In agile projects, work items, user stories, and related tracking documents preserve valuable traces of that intent, but they rarely support direct architectural analysis. This work investigates the recovery of C4 architecture diagrams from historical agile work items using an LLM-based pipeline. The semi-automatic five-step workflow employs a prompt chain, bidirectional traceability, and Chain-of-Thought reasoning to transform unstructured Azure DevOps work items into visual artifacts. Evaluated on two industry projects, we use a mixed-methods design combining qualitative expert interviews with a quantitative stability analysis. Practitioners perceive the generated architectural baselines as accurate and highly useful for system comprehension. Strictly bound by their input data, the artifacts mirror the documented intent, thereby surfacing discrepancies and architectural drift when compared to the implemented reality. Quantitatively, the workflow exhibits high stability for architectural entities but lower stability for their relationships, with relative variance compounding across generation steps. The proposed workflow demonstrates the practical viability of LLM-assisted architectural recovery based on development process artifacts.

</details>


### [95] [On the Maintenance and Co-evolution of Agent Plugins: An Empirical Study of Claude Code Plugin Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28497)
*Ahmed Hereiz,Yingzhe Lyu,Hao Li,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对1,926个Claude Code插件市场仓库中的8,351个插件和77,773次提交进行了实证研究，发现该市场快速增长，插件开发以功能驱动为主（占比39.6%，远超传统开源软件的17.2%），Claude参与34.9%的提交，且技能目录中自然语言指令文件与实现脚本以高于随机水平的速度共同演化，形成传统软件工程中未见的新型维护依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理插件市场正在迅速扩展，但这些新兴仓库的结构、维护和共同演化动态尚未得到实证探究。与传统软件包通过源代码交付功能不同，代理插件通过自然语言指令文件、脚本和配置文件的组合交付功能，因此需要研究这些插件是持续维护的制品还是一次性编写的临时制品。

Method: 对1,926个托管Claude Code插件市场的GitHub仓库进行大规模实证研究，分析8,351个插件和77,773次提交，覆盖2,018个市场，通过提交类型分类、组件类型关联分析和共同变迁分析等方法，研究插件市场的结构、维护模式及组件间的共同演化关系。

Result: 市场在2025年10月发布后六个月内插件相关提交活动增长8.8倍；软件工程任务类插件占61.3%；功能驱动提交占比39.6%，是传统开源软件（17.2%）的两倍以上；Claude共同编写34.9%的提交；文档、性能、样式和重构四类提交在插件仓库中与传统软件语义显著不同；技能目录中指令文件和实现脚本以高于随机水平共同演化，其中78%的共同变化具有功能耦合。

Conclusion: AI代理插件市场快速扩张且以功能驱动开发为主，插件是持续维护的制品而非一次性制品。技能目录中指令文件与脚本的功能耦合共同演化代表了传统软件工程中不存在的全新维护依赖类型，为理解AI代理生态系统的软件工程实践提供了新的视角。

Abstract: AI coding agents, software tools that automate development tasks through reasoning and tool use, are increasingly extended through plugin marketplaces, yet the structure, maintenance, and co-evolution dynamics of these emerging repositories remain empirically unexplored. Unlike traditional software packages that deliver functionality through source code, agent plugins deliver functionality through a combination of natural-language instruction files, scripts, and configuration files, raising the question of whether these plugins are maintained artifacts that co-evolve across components, or one-off artifacts that developers write once and do not need to revisit. To study the maintenance and co-evolution of agent plugins, we conduct an empirical study of 1,926 repositories hosting Claude Code plugin marketplaces, analyzing 8,351 plugins and 77,773 commits across 2,018 marketplaces. We find that the marketplace is expanding rapidly, plugin-touching commit activity growing 8.8x over six months after the October 2025 launch, and plugins targeting Software Engineering tasks accounting for 61.3% of all plugins. Plugin development is predominantly feature-driven, with feature commits occurring at more than twice the rate of conventional open-source software (OSS) (39.6% vs. 17.2%). Claude co-authors 34.9% of all commits, and four commit types (docs, perf, style, and refactor) carry substantially different meanings in plugin repositories than in traditional software. Most component types evolve independently, but within skills directories, natural-language instruction files and implementation scripts co-evolve at above-chance rates, with 78% of co-changes being functionally coupled, representing a new class of maintenance dependency not observed in traditional software engineering.

</details>


### [96] [A System-of-Systems Case Study for the Verification of Composed Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28498)
*Mennatullah T. Khedr,Mengwei Xu,John S. Fitzgerald,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了系统之系统（SoS）环境中数字孪生（DT）验证与确认的挑战，通过在VDM-RT中建模一个温室SoS案例，探讨了DT质量属性（如相关性、可验证性、可替代性和保真度）的正式化方法，并分析了在SoS层面组合这些工件时遇到的障碍。研究发现DT质量的解释需适应不同架构角色，局部质量保证构成组合推理的假设，完整的SoS组合形式化验证被确定为未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生工程方法缺乏关于如何形式化和验证相关性、可验证性、可替代性和保真度等质量属性的实用指导，这一需求在依赖DT组合的系统之系统（SoS）环境中尤为迫切。因此需要一个DT验证与确认框架来应对这一挑战。

Method: 采用案例研究方法：在VDM-RT中构建DT赋能的温室SoS可执行形式化模型，提出基于属性的DT质量形式化描述，将DT质量操作化为可验证的属性集合，并分析在SoS级别组合这些工件时出现的障碍，同时识别支持验证的形式化建模分析技术和工具。

Result: 研究揭示了DT质量的解释必须适应不同架构角色，局部质量保证构成组合推理的假设条件；同时识别出在SoS层面组合DT工件时的具体障碍，并确认完整的形式化验证SoS组合仍需未来工作。

Conclusion: 面向SoS的DT验证与确认框架应解决质量属性的架构角色依赖性以及局部到整体的组合推理问题；当前研究为DT质量属性的形式化和验证迈出了第一步，但完整的SoS组合形式化验证尚待进一步探索。

Abstract: Current approaches to engineering dependable Digital Twins (DTs) of Cyber-Physical Systems lack practical guidance on how qualities such as relevance, verifiability, substitutability and fidelity may be formalised and verified. This need is amplified in Systems-of-Systems (SoS), where reliance is placed on the composition of DTs. The goal of this study is to identify foundational challenges that a framework for DT validation and verification should address in an SoS setting, and to investigate the formalisation of individual DT artefacts as a step towards compositional reasoning. We present a case study based on a DT-enabled greenhouse SoS modelled in VDM-RT (Vienna Development Method, Real-Time), including executable formal models, a property-based account of DT qualities, and an analysis of the obstacles arising when attempting to compose these artefacts at the SoS level. We consider how DT qualities may be operationalised as sets of verifiable properties. Formal modelling and analysis techniques and tools supporting their verification are identified. The study reveals how interpretations of DT qualities must adapt to different architectural roles and how local quality guarantees form assumptions for compositional reasoning. Full formalisation and verification of SoS composition is identified as future work.

</details>


### [97] [Rethinking Vulnerability Remediation as a Capacity Allocation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28509)
*Jana Stucke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将漏洞修复视为流量控制问题而非单纯的优先级排序问题，通过Apache Jira、Mozilla Bugzilla等多个真实数据源分析发现，漏洞到达队列普遍处于或超过容量上限，容量分配和队列状态转换比预测排名更能影响修复效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI加速漏洞发现，修复吞吐量可能成为比优先级排序精度更严重的瓶颈。现有研究大多关注如何提高漏洞优先级排序的准确性，但忽略了修复流程本身的容量约束问题。

Method: 利用Apache Jira、Mozilla Bugzilla、Red Hat安全勘误、五个公共Jira组织和npm依赖图等数据源，将漏洞修复建模为流控制问题，分析队列容量状态、严重性排序策略、容量预留等对不同系统的修复时间和关键漏洞延迟的影响，并进行所有者层面的容量分析。

Result: Apache解析时间呈强重尾分布，94-100%的到达进入估计达到或超过容量的队列；队列上下文模型预测判别力中等，与简单项目基线相当；严重性到速度的区分度在不同系统中差异显著。流控制分析显示，从过载到排空队列的转换与更短的解析时间相关，严重性优先排序在固定容量下减少关键项目延迟，容量预留可减少长期关键项目延迟。所有者层面分析表明容量只有在需求发生地或可通过相关专业连接转移时才有效。

Conclusion: 漏洞修复应被重新定位为流量控制和容量分配问题，而非单纯的排名问题。有效的修复策略需要关注容量在哪里、如何分配以及在何处可转移，而不是仅优化优先级排序算法。

Abstract: As AI accelerates vulnerability discovery, remediation throughput may become a greater constraint than prioritisation accuracy. This study evaluates vulnerability remediation as a flow-control problem using Apache Jira, Mozilla Bugzilla, Red Hat security errata, five public Jira organisations, and an npm dependency graph. Apache resolution times are strongly heavy-tailed, while 94-100% of arrivals in the primary issue trackers enter queues estimated to be at or above capacity. Queue-context models provide only moderate predictive discrimination and are largely matched by simple project-level baselines. Severity-to-speed discrimination varies substantially across systems. Flow-control analyses show larger operational effects: transitions from overloaded to draining queues are associated with shorter resolution times, severity-first sequencing reduces critical-item delay at fixed capacity, and capacity reservation can reduce prolonged critical-item delays. Owner-level analyses further show that available capacity is useful only when it is located where demand occurs or can be transferred through relevant expertise connections. These findings support treating vulnerability remediation as a flow-control and capacity-allocation problem rather than solely a ranking problem.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Time Capsule of Testable Human Knowledge: 41 Years of Jeopardy! in a Single Free Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27459)
*David Noever,Forrest McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比2011年IBM Watson的DeepQA系统与现代开放权重大语言模型（Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit量化）在完整Jeopardy!题库上的表现，论证了问答型知识快照已从昂贵、固化的服务器集群时代演进为便携、免费且持续更新的时代。模型在529,939条线索上达到67.0%的准确率，且在训练截止后的新线索上仍保持65%的准确率，而Watson在同类数据上为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示知识型AI系统的范式转变：2011年Watson代表了不可移动、不可复制、冻结于构建时刻的知识快照，而现代语言模型将同样的文化问答知识封装为便携文件。同时作者希望挑战"训练数据暴露是语言模型特有缺陷"的偏见，指出Watson的语料库本质上也是为Jeopardy定制训练的。

Method: 使用单个9GB的开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-14B，4位量化），在完整开放Jeopardy!线索数据集（529,939条，覆盖1984-2025年41个播出季）上进行严格强制回答协议测试，采用精确与模糊匹配评估；并额外评估训练截止日期之后播出线索上的表现，与Watson和Claude Opus 4.8进行对比。

Result: 模型在全部线索上达到67.0%的准确率，事实型类别上超过85%；在训练截止后播出的线索上保持65%的准确率，Claude Opus 4.8达到95%，而Watson由于构建时数据冻结得分为零。这是首次有模型在完整Jeopardy!语料库上运行。

Conclusion: 现代开放权重语言模型已实现了Watson时代无法企及的知识可移植性与时效性：同样的"文化可回答快照"如今是便携且近乎免费的，且不像Watson那样冻结于自身时代。知识问答能力从专用硬件集群转移到了可封存于时间胶囊的单个文件中。

Abstract: In 2011, IBM's Watson was something like a sealed capsule of its era's queryable knowledge. Its DeepQA system defeated the strongest human Jeopardy! champions, but the knowledge that let it do so lived in a curated billion-document corpus running on a cluster of POWER7 servers, frozen at build time and impossible to move or copy. We show that the same kind of artifact, a snapshot of what a culture can answer, is now portable and essentially free. We evaluate a single 9 GB open-weight model (Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit) against the complete open Jeopardy! clue dataset, 529,939 clues across all 41 broadcast seasons from 1984 to 2025. To our knowledge this is the first time a model has been run over the full corpus. The 41 years mark only how long the questions were collected. What they test is far older and broader: the accumulated body of human general knowledge a culture considers worth knowing, from ancient history and dead languages to science, literature, and geography, with a verified answer for every item. The model answers 67.0% of all clues under a strict forced-response protocol with exact and fuzzy matching, and exceeds 85% on factoid categories. We treat training-data exposure as something both systems share rather than a flaw unique to language models. Watson's case is in fact the more extreme one. Its corpus was assembled to contain Jeopardy answers and it was tuned on past clues, and it could not answer anything outside that curated distribution. The decisive test is whether a model can answer clues that did not exist when it was built. On clues aired after its training cutoff, the local model holds 65% and Claude Opus 4.8 holds 95%, while Watson by construction scores zero. The capability survives the move from a server room to a file you could seal in a time capsule, and unlike Watson it is not frozen to its own moment.

</details>


### [99] [Rating the Raters: Rasch Measurement Theory for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27463)
*Pratik S. Sachdeva,Nathan Boudol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将Rasch测量理论（RMT）应用于LLM评估范式（LLM作为考生、评委、评分员），通过多面Rasch模型对九种LLM的评分进行分解，揭示LLM与人类评分员在严重程度、项目标定、问题顺序鲁棒性、目标身份敏感性和评分量表使用等方面的系统性差异，而这些差异在标准评估实践中会被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已同时扮演被评估者、评估者和评审者三重角色，但标准评估实践往往忽略测量中各核心组件（潜在属性、测试项目、评分员）对结果的贡献，导致我们无法充分理解究竟在测量什么。这一测量模糊性问题阻碍了对LLM评估结果的深入解读。

Method: 将LLM评估视为测量问题，采用Rasch测量理论（RMT）框架，对"LLM作为评分员"范式进行案例研究。使用Measuring Hate Speech语料库（其构念本身基于RMT构建），对九种覆盖不同家族和能力水平的LLM的标注数据拟合一系列多面Rasch模型（many-facet Rasch models），将序数评分分解为可分离的测量面。

Result: 分析表明LLM与人类评分员在多个维度上存在系统性差异：评分严重程度（severity）、项目级标定（item-level calibration）、问题顺序鲁棒性、目标身份敏感性和评分量表使用方式。这些差异在标准评估实践中均会被掩盖，只有通过RMT才能被揭示。

Conclusion: RMT应被纳入LLM评估工具包，适用于LLM作为考生、评委和评分员三种范式的评估分析。RMT不仅能提供可分离的测量面分解，还提供一系列诊断工具来识别失准测量和评分员偏差，从而为LLM评估提供更严谨的测量学基础。

Abstract: LLMs now sit on every side of evaluation: as examinees scored on benchmarks, judges of other models' outputs, and raters of human-generated content. Each paradigm can be viewed as a measurement problem, where a latent property of an object is probed with items from an instrument (e.g., benchmark) by raters. Standard evaluation practices often neglect the contributions of each core component to the end result, limiting our understanding of what is being measured. Rasch measurement theory (RMT) is well-suited to this kind of problem. RMT decomposes ordinal ratings into separable facets on a common scale. It further provides a battery of diagnostics that can identify miscalibrated measurements and rater biases. We present a case study of RMT applied to the LLM-as-rater paradigm using the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, whose construct was itself built under RMT. We fit a series of many-facet Rasch models to annotations from nine LLMs spanning families and capability levels. Our analyses show that LLMs systematically differ from human raters in severity, item-level calibration, question-order robustness, target-identity sensitivity, and rating scale use, which all would be obscured by standard evaluation practice. Overall, we argue that RMT belongs in the toolkit for evaluating LLM-as-examinee, -judge, and -rater paradigms.

</details>


### [100] [Not All Explanations Are Sought: Information-Seeking Psychology for Human-Centered XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27464)
*Andrea Beretta,Salvatore Rinzivillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以人为本的可解释AI（HCXAI）应融入信息寻求心理学，基于Sharot和Sunstein的动机框架，从工具性、享乐性和认知性三类预期效用来理解用户是否愿意与解释互动，并利用认知偏见分析过度寻求与不足寻求两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCXAI研究主要关注如何生成更好的解释，却忽略了用户为何及何时真正想要寻求解释这一核心问题。对于日益复杂的智能体AI系统，解释不仅要帮助理解单一输出，还需支持级联行动的预测、风险评估与干预决策，因此需要从心理学视角理解用户的信息寻求动机。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的方法，整合Sharot和Sunstein的信息寻求动机框架，提出三类预期效用（工具性、享乐性、认知性）作为用户决定是否参与解释的依据；并通过六种认知偏见（控制幻觉、自动化偏见、不切实际乐观、影响偏见、过度自信、确认偏见）解释效用估计为何存在偏差，进而识别过量和不足两类信息寻求失败模式。

Result: 识别出两类信息寻求失败模式：过度信息寻求会碎片化注意力而不改善决策，不足信息寻求会遗漏关键风险与误解；尤其在智能体AI场景中，这些问题更为突出，因为解释需要支撑更为复杂的级联行动理解和干预判断。

Conclusion: 主张HCXAI应从"使解释可获得"转向"使解释被寻求"，即设计系统时需考虑用户实际想要知道的事情、时机和原因，将信息寻求心理学融入可解释AI的设计原则。

Abstract: This position paper argues that human-centered explainable AI (HCXAI) should incorporate insights from the psychology of information seeking. Drawing on Sharot and Sunstein's framework of information-seeking motives, we propose that people evaluate whether to engage with explanations based on three types of expected utility: instrumental (will it help me act better?), hedonic (will it make me feel better?), and cognitive (will it improve my understanding?). Each utility is estimated through a lens shaped by well-documented cognitive biases, including illusion of control, automation bias, unrealistic optimism, impact bias, overconfidence, and confirmation bias. These biases can lead to two failure modes: excessive information-seeking that fragments attention without improving decisions, and insufficient information-seeking that leaves critical risks and misunderstandings unexamined. This challenge is particularly acute for agentic AI systems, where explanations must support not just understanding a single output but anticipating cascading actions, assessing risks, and deciding when to intervene. By integrating information-seeking psychology into HCXAI, we advocate for a shift from making explanations available to making them sought: designing systems that account for when and why users actually want to know.

</details>


### [101] [Retrieving Relations, Detecting Fallacies: A RAG Approach to Political Debate Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27471)
*Deborah Dore,Greta Damo,Elena Cabrio,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于引导式检索增强（guided retrieval-augmented）的谬误检测与分类方法，利用论证结构中的支持与攻击关系动态引导文档检索，在ElecDeb60to20基准上实现宏F1高达0.864（检测）和0.725（分类）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谬误检测在高风险场景（如政治辩论）中至关重要，但仅依赖表面文本不足以识别谬误，需要世界知识和论证间关系。已有工作虽证明论证结构有助于分类，但仅作为静态分类特征，灵活性受限。

Method: 提出一种引导式检索增强方法，利用论证关系（支持与攻击）动态引导相关文档的检索，而非将结构作为静态特征。在15GB政治文档知识库上进行42种检索配置和14个模型的系统评估。

Result: 在ElecDeb60to20基准上，谬误检测宏F1提升至0.864，分类宏F1提升至0.725，均超越非检索基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当检索过程经过论证结构引导时，引入外部知识能显著增强谬误检测与分类性能。

Abstract: Fallacies are arguments that employ invalid reasoning, making their automatic detection critical in sensitive contexts such as high-stakes political debates, where public opinion is shaped. Spotting a fallacious argument requires contextual knowledge beyond its pure surface text. This entails world knowledge pertaining to the subject matter under discussion, as well as knowledge of the relationships that exist between arguments within the argumentative discourse. Prior work on fallacy analysis has shown that argumentative discourse structure can beneficially improve classification performance. However, such structure is typically encoded only as static classifier features, limiting its flexibility. Building on this intuition while addressing this limitation, we introduce a guided retrieval-augmented methodology for fallacy detection and classification that leverages argumentative relations of support and attack to dynamically steer the extraction of relevant documents. We evaluate our approach on the ElecDeb60to20 benchmark across 42 retrieval configurations and 14 models, performing retrieval over a 15GB knowledge base of collected political-related documents. Our approach improves macro-F1 up to 0.864 for fallacy detection and up to 0.725 for classification over non-retrieval baselines. These results show that incorporating external knowledge significantly enhances fallacy detection and classification when retrieval is argumentatively guided.

</details>


### [102] [LLM-Augmented Causal Discovery: Probabilistic Fusion of Edge Existence and Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27472)
*Neville K. Kitson,Anthony Constantinou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率依赖图（PDG）表示方法，通过加权平均融合贝叶斯网络结构学习（BNSL）算法与大型语言模型（LLM）的因果知识，在26个基准网络上验证了这种融合方式能够显著提升因果图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNSL从观测数据中学习因果结构时面临方向可辨识性问题，而LLM虽然具备广泛的因果知识但往往不够可靠。两者恰好互补：BNSL擅长边存在性的高召回率，LLM擅长边的方向准确性。然而如何有效地融合这两类来源的因果信息仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出概率依赖图（Probabilistic Dependency Graphs, PDGs）作为新的表示形式。在PDG中，每条边关联一个关于有向、无向和不存在三种状态的分布，通过加权平均实现两种来源的融合。实验采用三个BNSL算法（FGES、Tabu、PC）与三个LLM（Gemini、Claude、GPT）的集成，在26个基准网络上评估50/50简单融合策略。

Result: 50/50融合在26个网络中的22个上优于任一单一来源的F1分数，平均提升0.056（p<0.001），具有统计显著性。分析显示两来源互补：BNSL提供高召回率的边骨架（80% vs LLM的60%），LLM提供高准确率的边方向（96% vs BNSL的77%）。

Conclusion: 将BNSL与LLM的因果信息表示为关于边存在性和方向的概率不确定性，并通过加权平均融合，是提升因果图准确性的实用且有效的方法。

Abstract: Bayesian network structure learning (BNSL) from observational data struggles with orientation identifiability, while large language models (LLMs) offer broad but often unreliable causal knowledge. We propose combining these complementary sources through a novel representation, termed Probabilistic Dependency Graphs (PDGs). In a PDG, each edge is associated with a distribution over directed, undirected, and absent states, enabling fusion via weighted averaging. We evaluate this approach on 26 benchmark networks, combining ensembles of three BNSL algorithms (FGES, Tabu, PC) with three LLMs (Gemini, Claude, GPT) across multiple prompts and random seeds. A simple 50/50 fusion improves F1 over the better of either source alone in 22 of 26 networks, with a statistically significant mean improvement of $0.056$ $(p<0.001)$. Analysis reveals that the two sources play complementary roles: BNSL contributes a high-recall edge skeleton (80\% vs 60\% for LLM), while LLM contributes accurate edge orientation (96\% vs 77\% for BNSL). Our results show that representing both sources as probabilistic uncertainty over edge existence and orientation is a practical and effective way to improve causal graph accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine: A Scientific Agent for PDE Discovery with Unknown Spatial Coefficient Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27475)
*YuJie Huang,WenWu He,ZhuoEr Lin,Congcong Liu,Dong Liang,Zhuo-Xu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HER-PDE是一个科学智能体框架，用于在非均匀介质中联合发现偏微分方程（PDE）结构及其非参数空间系数场。该智能体通过提出完整的表达式树假设、评估与精化，从两条不同激励下的含噪轨迹中恢复异构控制律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀介质中发现PDE需要同时识别控制算子及其参数化的未知空间场，这两项任务相互耦合：场的变化会改变微分定律，而过于灵活的空间场可能掩盖单一轨迹上的结构误差。因此需要同时处理结构发现与系数场恢复的联合方法。

Method: HER-PDE框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 假设生成：智能体从两条不同激励产生的含噪轨迹中提出完整的表达式树假设；(2) 评估：假设评估接口（HEI）仅估计每个假设中明确声明的空间场，不添加缺失项，并通过双向交叉激励迁移对结构打分；(3) 精化：结合创造性结构探索与局部候选精化，最终在密封（未用于训练）的时间区间上对选定的定律进行审计。

Result: 在五个受控的二维系统中（含5%相对高斯状态噪声），智能体在所有五个案例中恢复了生成算子，包括等价符号场和乘积规则参数化。在九个未知系数场上，恢复的场达到中位Pearson相关系数约0.85，中位相对L2误差约0.28。

Conclusion: 智能体引导的假设精化方法可以在不为空间系数预设参数形式的情况下恢复异构控制律，证明了科学智能体在开放式的PDE发现任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Discovering PDEs in heterogeneous media requires jointly identifying the governing operator and the unknown spatial fields that parameterize it. These tasks are coupled: changing field placement changes the differential law, while a sufficiently flexible field can conceal structural error on a single trajectory. We present Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine for PDE Discovery (HER-PDE), a scientific-agent framework that discovers compositional PDE structure together with nonparametric, time-invariant coefficient fields. The Agent analyzes two noisy trajectories generated by different excitations, proposes complete expression-tree hypotheses, and combines creative structural exploration with local candidate refinement. Its Hypothesis Evaluation Interface (HEI) estimates only the fields explicitly declared in each hypothesis, never adds missing terms, and scores structures by bidirectional cross-excitation transfer. The selected law is subsequently audited on a sealed temporal interval. Across five controlled two-dimensional systems observed with 5 percent relative Gaussian state noise, the Agent recovers the generating operator in all five cases, including equivalent signed-field and product-rule parameterizations. Across nine unknown coefficient fields, the recovered fields attain a median Pearson correlation of approximately 0.85 and a median relative L2 error of approximately 0.28. These results show that agent-guided hypothesis refinement can recover heterogeneous governing laws without prescribing a parametric form for their spatial coefficients.

</details>


### [104] [Class-Based Heuristic Selection for Solving the Flying Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27476)
*Sanyar Ahmadi,Pedram Asadzadeh,Amanj Khorramian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于类别的启发式A*算法（CBHA*），用于解决受结构性约束的空间规划问题。该算法通过集成通用移动约束、形式化运动学分类（将状态空间划分为七类）和类别条件式平局打破机制，在两列飞行方块拼图基准上实现了93.4%的成功率，节点扩展量减少87.98%，平均有效分支因子约为3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用启发式搜索方法在受限空间域中无法利用领域结构约束，导致在困难实例上搜索性能急剧退化。作者希望通过两列飞行方块拼图这一NP完全的微世界来揭示瓶颈几何（间隙-尺寸约束）与多智能体路径规划、自动驾驶导航、块重定位等系统中的共性结构，从而开发更高效的空间规划算法。

Method: CBHA*算法包含三个核心组件：(1) 通用移动约束（General Move Constraint），捕获空地稀缺时的最小位移成本；(2) 形式化运动学分类法，将状态空间划分为七个互斥类别，基于空缺率和目标方块几何提供可证明可采纳的启发式；(3) 类别条件式平局打破机制，在深度优先与垂直距离排序之间动态切换以克服f值平台期。

Result: 在146个基准实例上，CBHA*达到93.4%的成功率，显著优于深度优先A*（64%）、标准A*（39%）和BFS（17%）；相较于标准A*减少了87.98%的节点扩展量，并保持约3的有效平均分支因子。

Conclusion: 类别触发的自适应启发式是高效空间规划的一种有原则性（principled）的机制，能够结构性泛化到物理约束系统，为受限空间域中的自主规划提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Heuristic search underlies planning in autonomous systems ranging from warehouse logistics to robotic navigation, yet generic heuristics fail to exploit the structural constraints that govern constrained spatial domains, causing search performance to degrade catastrophically on harder instances. We study this problem through the two-column Flying Block Puzzle, a rigorously NP-complete spatial planning microworld whose bottleneck geometry mirrors clearance-to-size constraints encountered in multi-agent path finding, autonomous vehicle navigation, and block relocation systems. We introduce the Class-Based Heuristic A* (CBHA*) algorithm, which integrates a General Move Constraint to capture minimum displacement costs when vacant units are scarce, a formal kinematic taxonomy partitioning the state space into seven mutually exclusive classes with provably admissible heuristics based on vacancy ratio and goal-piece geometry, and a class-conditional tie-breaking mechanism that dynamically switches between depth-priority and vertical-distance ordering to overcome f-value plateaus. Over 146 benchmark instances, CBHA* achieves a 93.4% success rate against 64% for Depth-Prioritized A*, 39% for Standard A*, and 17% for BFS, while reducing node expansions by 87.98% relative to Standard A* and sustaining an average effective branching factor of approximately 3, demonstrating that class-triggered adaptive heuristics constitute a principled mechanism for efficient spatial planning that generalizes structurally to physical constraint systems.

</details>


### [105] [Benchmarking General Mobile Assistants in Challenging Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27477)
*Yiqi Zhu,Feiyu Gao,Jiaxing Fan,Jiahui Zeng,Minggang Wu,Chenliang Li,Haiyang Xu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GMA基准，用于评估通用移动助手在真实世界复杂场景中的表现，包含7个基于开源项目的应用、300个跨四个难度等级的任务，并对8个前沿模型进行评测。结果显示任务复杂度越高性能下降越明显，且合理的代理框架设计（如上下文保持和显式状态跟踪）能显著提升性能，但效果因基础模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AndroidWorld和MobileWorld等基准为移动代理评估提供了良好基础，但其应用覆盖面和任务设计尚未充分反映真实移动使用的多样性和复杂性，需要更全面的评估基准。

Method: GMA基准包含7个基于开源项目的应用（覆盖生活分享和旅行规划等领域）、300个任务，分为四个难度等级（从原子操作到复杂多步工作流）。作者评估了8个前沿模型，并在共享环境、模型设置和任务分类下进行受控消融研究，考察代理框架选择（包括上下文保持和显式状态跟踪）的影响。

Result: 任务复杂度增加时，模型性能显著下降，当前代理仍远不能可靠处理真实用户需求。消融研究表明，适当的代理框架设计可在复杂工作流中显著提升性能，但具体设计的效果因基础模型不同而有所差异。

Conclusion: GMA补充了现有基准在应用覆盖面和任务复杂度上的不足，为移动代理评估提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台，同时也为研究框架设计如何支持复杂移动工作流中的可靠执行提供了基础。

Abstract: Graphical user interfaces have emerged as an important environment for evaluating autonomous AI agents on multimodal interactive tasks. Existing benchmarks such as AndroidWorld and MobileWorld provide strong foundations for mobile agent evaluation, but their application coverage and task design do not yet fully capture the diversity and complexity of realistic mobile use. We present GMA, a benchmark for evaluating general mobile assistants in challenging real-world scenarios. GMA introduces seven applications based on open-source projects, spanning domains such as lifestyle sharing and travel planning, and 300 tasks across four difficulty tiers, from atomic actions to complex multi-step workflows. We evaluate eight frontier models and find that performance declines substantially as task complexity increases, with current agents remaining far from reliably handling realistic user requirements. We further conduct controlled ablation studies of agent harness choices, including context retention and explicit state tracking, under a shared environment, model setting, and task taxonomy. Results show that appropriate harness design can meaningfully improve performance, particularly on demanding workflows, while the effectiveness of specific designs can vary across foundation models. Overall, GMA complements existing benchmarks by expanding application coverage and task complexity, providing a challenging testbed for evaluating mobile agents and studying how harness design supports reliable execution in complex mobile workflows.

</details>


### [106] [Effectiveness of IoT and Deep Learning for Detection and Severity Assessment of Postelectrotermes militaris in Tea Plantations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27480)
*D. K. C. Senevirathna,A. A. E. Nanayakkara,H. M. C. K. Kulathunga,J. K. D. P. Nadula,R. M. Mapatuna,Malithi Nawarathne,Jaliya L. Wijayaraja,P. D. Senanayake,Samitha Vidhanaarachchi,Kalpani Manathunga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于物联网（IoT）声学监测与深度学习相结合的框架，用于茶园中Upcountry活木白蚁（ULWT）侵害的早期检测与严重程度评估。通过Raspberry Pi IoT设备采集茶树干音频信号，利用傅里叶变换得到的频谱图训练CNN进行侵害分类，并结合侵害概率、声学振幅和邻近受害植株构建加权严重度模型，实现空间分布可视化。在野外测试中，CNN在测试集上达到了81.5%的准确率和0.819的ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 茶园易受Postelectrotermes militaris（Upcountry活木白蚁，ULWT）侵害，若侵害未被及时发现会造成重大损失。现有方法缺乏早期检测和严重程度评估手段，需要一种非侵入性、可持续的监测方案。

Method: 采用高灵敏度麦克风连接Raspberry Pi IoT设备，非侵入式采集茶树干音频信号并记录地理坐标。音频数据经过修剪、重采样和分割后，获得2,000个10秒样本（1,000健康+1,000受侵害），按1,600/200/200划分训练/验证/测试集。利用傅里叶变换生成的频谱图训练CNN进行侵害分类和概率估计。提出加权严重度模型，综合CNN侵害概率、平均声学振幅和5米范围内的邻近受害植株数，并通过地理空间映射可视化侵害分布。

Result: 在斯里兰卡Pundaluoya受ULWT侵害的茶园进行的野外试验证明了该框架在真实环境噪声下的可行性。在独立测试集上，CNN达到81.5%的准确性、80.6%的精确率、83.0%的召回率、81.8%的F1分数和0.819的ROC-AUC。此外，框架超越了二元侵害检测，实现了基于侵害概率、声学振幅和邻近受害植株的定量严重度评估。

Conclusion: 该框架提供的严重度和地理空间输出能够帮助茶园管理者识别高风险区域、优先安排现场检查，并实施更及时、有针对性的防治措施。数据集已在Kaggle公开，支持后续研究。

Abstract: Tea plantations are vulnerable to Postelectrotermes militaris, commonly known as the Upcountry Live Wood Termite (ULWT), which can cause substantial damage when infestations remain undetected. This study proposes an IoT-enabled acoustic monitoring framework integrated with deep learning for early detection and severity assessment of ULWT infestations in tea plantations.
  Research Method: Audio signals were captured non-invasively from tea trunks using a high-sensitivity microphone connected to a Raspberry Pi-based IoT device, with geographic coordinates recorded for spatial tracking. After trimming, resampling, and segmentation, 2,000 ten-second samples were obtained, comprising 1,000 healthy and 1,000 infested samples, and divided into 1,600 training, 200 validation, and 200 test samples. The dataset used in this study is publicly available on Kaggle (Senevirathna et al. 2026). Fourier-derived spectrograms trained a CNN for infestation classification and probability estimation. A weighted severity model combined CNN probability, mean acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants within 5 m, with geospatial mapping used to visualize infestation distribution.
  Findings and Values: Field trials in a ULWT-affected tea plantation in Pundaluoya demonstrated feasibility under realistic environmental noise. On the held-out test set, the CNN achieved 81.5% accuracy, 80.6% precision, 83.0% recall, 81.8% F1-score, and 0.819 ROC-AUC. Beyond binary infestation detection, the framework introduced quantitative severity assessment using infestation probability, acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants. The resulting severity and geospatial outputs can support plantation managers in identifying high-risk areas, prioritizing field inspections, and implementing more timely and targeted control measures.

</details>


### [107] [Context Localization for Generalized Level-Based Evaluation in Knowledge-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27482)
*Ondrej Hutník,Natália Puškárová*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于知识系统中广义分级评估的上下文定位问题，提出一个框架，其中定义在事实、规则、案例、标准或证据单元上的结构化非负分数通过可接受知识上下文上的条件聚合测试进行评估。广义级别度量在达到规定级别的所有上下文中最大化一个单调集函数。作者刻画了何时通过上下文B过滤分数等价于通过B交集来定位可接受上下文，并证明了该一致性对所有单调集函数成立当且仅当两个结构条件满足：对上下文的单调性以及排除B外正局部支持的约简性质。文章还分析了产生约简性质的逐点和分块生成机制，将结果扩展到参数化系统，并将其解释为上下文依赖证据选择、非加性支持评估和级别基知识聚合的稳定性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在知识系统中，评估通常依赖于特定上下文，而上下文的局部化（localization）如何与分数过滤（filtering）保持一致是一个基础性问题。作者希望建立统一的数学框架，刻画上下文定位与分数过滤操作等价的条件，从而为上下文依赖的证据选择和非加性支持评估提供理论基础。

Method: 本文采用公理化方法，定义一个广义级别度量——在可接受上下文集合上最大化单调集函数以达到规定级别。通过比较两种操作（按上下文B过滤分数 vs. 通过B交集定位上下文）的等价性，推导出两个必要且充分的结构条件：对上下文的单调性（monotonicity with respect to contexts）和约简性质（reduction property）。进一步分析逐点（pointwise）和分块（block-generated）两种生成机制，并将框架推广至参数化系统。

Result: 主定理证明：对所有单调集函数而言，过滤与定位的一致性等价于上述两个结构条件。该结果扩展到了参数化系统，并被解释为上下文依赖证据选择的稳定性判据。

Conclusion: 该工作为知识系统中的上下文定位与级别评估提供了严谨的充要条件刻画，阐明了过滤操作与上下文定位操作在何种结构条件下可互相替代，为上下文敏感的等级聚合与证据评估提供了重要的理论稳定性准则。

Abstract: We study context localization for generalized level-based evaluation in knowledge-based systems. The framework models situations where a structured nonnegative score, defined on facts, rules, cases, criteria or evidence units, is evaluated through conditional aggregation tests on admissible knowledge contexts. The generalized level measure maximizes a monotone set function over all contexts whose aggregated support reaches a prescribed level. We characterize when filtering the score by a context $B$ is equivalent to localizing the admissible contexts by intersection with $B$. The main theorem shows that this consistency holds for all monotone set functions if and only if two structural conditions are satisfied: monotonicity with respect to contexts and a reduction property excluding positive localized support outside $B$. We analyze pointwise and block-generated mechanisms producing the reduction property, extend the result to parameterized systems, and interpret it as a stability criterion for context-dependent evidence selection, non-additive support evaluation and level-based knowledge aggregation.

</details>


### [108] [CareGraph: An Auditable Hybrid AI Framework for Evidence-Grounded Personalized Longitudinal Health Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27484)
*Pratik Ghawate,Tanvi Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CareGraph是一个可审计的混合AI框架，将临床、患者自报和可穿戴设备等异构健康记录转化为优先级趋势、缺失上下文指标、有界下一步行动、讨论问题及溯源解释。它不做诊断、预测或自主临床决策，而是通过确定性分析、上下文检测、图构建、约束语言模型合成、证据验证、安全控制和发布门控的流水线来组织证据。测试表明其在趋势规则、缺失上下文检测（strict micro F1 0.815 vs 0.318）、安全规则集（F1 0.974）和审计能力上表现优异，且相比GPT 5.6更快、输出更短、词汇对齐更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化医疗领域正在被AI转型，但临床数据、自我报告数据和可穿戴设备数据等异构证据碎片化严重，难以解读和追踪，缺乏一个既能整合证据又具备可审计性和安全边界的框架。

Method: 提出CareGraph混合AI框架，采用包含六个阶段的流水线：确定性分析、上下文检测、图构建、约束语言模型合成、证据验证、安全控制与发布门控。框架以图结构组织证据并附带溯源链接，设计上明确不做诊断、预测、治疗选择或自主决策。测试使用4组各400名患者的合成队列（开发、验证、留出）以及作者构建的基准集进行评估。

Result: 留出数据上，冻结的OLS趋势规则配充分性门控达到0.827准确率、0.837宏F1（95%CI: 0.819-0.854）、0.974不足数据F1。缺失上下文检测strict micro F1为0.815（旧检测器仅0.318）。安全规则集v1.2达到1.000精确率、0.950召回率、0.974 F1。80名患者的审计中79个综合和78个演示无回退，1个被阻断、1个因无效证据键安全失败关闭。相比GPT 5.6（56名匹配患者），CareGraph更快（40.15秒 vs 49.62秒）、更短（661 vs 1163词）、探索性词汇对齐更好，但基线使用更少token并引用更多原始证据。

Conclusion: CareGraph为智能个性化健康系统提供了安全有界的基础架构，在证据组织、可审计性和安全控制方面表现良好，但增量图对生成的影响仍需配对评估以进一步验证其价值。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming personalized healthcare, yet fragmented clinical, self reported, and wearable evidence remains difficult to interpret and trace. We present CareGraph, an auditable hybrid AI framework that converts heterogeneous records into prioritized trends, missing context indicators, bounded next steps, discussion questions, and provenance linked explanations. CareGraph organizes evidence without diagnosing, predicting outcomes, selecting treatment, or making autonomous clinical decisions. Its pipeline covers deterministic analysis, context detection, graph construction, constrained language model synthesis, evidence validation, safety controls, and release gating. Tests used synthetic cohorts of 400 patients each for development, validation, and holdout. On holdout data, a frozen ordinary least squares trend rule with a sufficiency gate achieved 0.827 accuracy, 0.837 macro F1 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.819 to 0.854, and 0.974 insufficient data F1. Missing context detection achieved 0.815 strict micro F1 versus 0.318 for the legacy detector. On an authored holdout benchmark, safety ruleset version 1.2 achieved 1.000 precision, 0.950 recall, and 0.974 F1. An audit requiring graph retrieval across 80 patients yielded 79 syntheses and 78 presentations without fallback; one output was blocked and one failed closed because of an invalid evidence key. Against monolithic GPT 5.6 on 56 matched patients, CareGraph was faster at 40.15 versus 49.62 seconds, shorter at 661 versus 1,163 words, and showed better exploratory lexical alignment with longitudinal targets; the baseline used fewer tokens and cited more raw evidence. Graph auditing verified provenance and deterministic retrieval; incremental graph effects on generation require paired evaluation. CareGraph offers a safety bounded foundation for intelligent personalized health systems.

</details>


### [109] [An Empirical Evaluation of Cross-City POI Recommendation on a Large-Scale Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27840)
*Peibo Li,Yang Song,Hao Xue,Maarten de Rijke,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用大规模基准Trip World重新审视跨城市兴趣点（POI）推荐任务，发现现有SOTA方法在全球化覆盖、低家乡-目的地重叠和大规模语义丰富的POI清单下存在三大瓶颈：家乡感知模型过度依赖目的地区域先验而非用户偏好迁移、精度-效率权衡在大规模下退化（最简单模型反而最强）、语义元数据整合机制收益甚微。此外，从下一POI推荐迁移的智能体方法在朴素适配下甚至不如简单的流行度先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市POI推荐对在不熟悉城市中导航至关重要，但以往研究受限于小规模数据基准，所得到的结论是否在真实世界的大规模、全球化场景下依然成立尚不明确。

Method: 利用新提出的大规模基准Trip World进行实证评估，系统性地重新检验代表性SOTA方法，并额外设计了基于下一POI推荐适配的智能体方法诊断性试点实验。

Result: 发现三大瓶颈：(1)家乡感知模型主要依赖目的地区域先验而非用户偏好迁移；(2)大规模下精度-效率权衡退化，最简单模型竞争力最强；(3)语义元数据整合机制收益有限。智能体方法朴素适配效果差于流行度先验。

Conclusion: 现有多数方法的结论难以直接推广到大尺度跨城市场景，亟需面向任务特性的设计，以支持跨城市偏好迁移、语义落地以及面向未知目的地库存的可扩展推理。

Abstract: Cross-city point-of-interest (POI) recommendation is crucial for navigating unfamiliar urban environments, yet its progress has historically been constrained by data limitations. Using the recently proposed large-scale benchmark Trip World, we empirically re-examine whether conclusions drawn on small prior benchmarks still hold under worldwide coverage, low home-destination region overlap, and large, semantically rich POI inventories. Our evaluation surfaces three bottlenecks of representative state-of-the-art methods: (1) hometown-aware models appear to rely more on destination-region priors than on user-specific preference transfer; (2) their accuracy-efficiency trade-off degrades at this scale, where the simplest model is among the strongest; and (3) existing mechanisms for integrating semantic metadata yield little benefit. We further include a diagnostic pilot on agentic methods adapted from next-POI recommendation, finding that naive adaptation trails a simple popularity prior even though the relevant semantic signal is present in the data. These results highlight the need for task-specific designs that support cross-city preference transfer, semantic grounding, and scalable reasoning over unseen destination inventories.

</details>


### [110] [Thinking Costs Tokens: When More Structure is Worth the Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27506)
*Thomas Nolasque,John Grey,Calista Pham,Ankit Vani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理结构（规划、验证、修复）在低token预算下是否会损害性能的问题。通过比较单体LLM与验证搜索架构在FinQA和TAT-QA金融推理任务上的表现，发现存在一个token预算阈值（约1,000-1,500输出等价token），低于该阈值时推理开销反而有害，高于该阈值时验证搜索架构持续优于单体模型（44% vs 40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理结构（搜索、验证、修订）可以提升语言模型的推理质量，但这些操作本身消耗预算。目前尚不清楚是否存在一个token预算阈值，低于该阈值时推理开销的代价超过其收益，高于该阈值时收益超过代价。回答这一问题对于合理分配推理预算、设计推理架构至关重要。

Method: 在FinQA和TAT-QA两个金融推理数据集上，使用GPT-5.4 mini模型，设计14个预算层级（250到42,000个输出等价token），比较两种系统：1）单体系统（单次LLM调用）；2）验证搜索架构（包含规划、标签盲检查、修复能力）。每种系统运行1,000个案例，共完成28,000个单元格。使用严格的交集-并集检验（intersection-union test）确认交叉点。

Result: 在最低两个层级（250和较低层级），两种系统均得0%（无法容纳完整提示）。在1,000 token时，单体达到18%准确率，而验证搜索接近0%（规划开销挤占了回答空间）。从1,500 token起，验证搜索持续超越单体，最高层级达到约44%，单体约40%。交叉点位于1,000到1,500输出等价token之间，交集-并集检验确认（两端p≤0.001）。

Conclusion: 存在明确的token预算阈值（约1,000-1,500 token），低于阈值时规划与验证的开销会损害性能，高于阈值时推理结构带来稳定的性能优势。这为推理预算分配提供了量化依据：在预算充足时值得采用结构化推理架构，而过低的预算下单体模型是更优选择。

Abstract: Adding inference structure to a language model lets it search, verify, and revise, but these actions consume the very budget they are supposed to use well. In this paper, we investigate whether there exists a token-budget threshold, below which the overhead of planning and verification hurts performance and above which it helps. We evaluate two systems on FinQA and TAT-QA financial reasoning tasks, using GPT-5.4 mini across 14 budget tiers ranging from 250 to 42,000 output-equivalent tokens. The first system is a monolith, which is a single LLM call. The second is a verified search architecture that adds planning, label-blind checking, and repair capabilities. We run 1,000 cases for a total of 28,000 completed cells. Both systems score 0% at the two lowest tiers, where neither can fit a complete prompt. At 1,000 tokens, the monolith reaches 18% accuracy while verified search scores near 0%, since the planning overhead leaves no room for an answer. From 1,500 tokens onward, verified search surpasses the monolith and maintains a consistent advantage, reaching approximately 44% at the highest tiers while the monolith reaches approximately 40%. The crossover occurs between 1,000 and 1,500 output-equivalent tokens, confirmed by a strict intersection-union test ($p \le 0.001$ at both endpoints).

</details>


### [111] [WM-R1: Training GUI Agents to Reason and leverage World Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27508)
*Yu Han,Tianwen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WM-R1，这是首个利用世界模型而非真实环境来训练移动GUI代理的强化学习框架。WM-R1在世界模型中进行所有rollout，并将世界模型嵌入推理过程，支持大规模并行和细粒度轨迹生成，配合多维规则奖励，在Android基准上显著优于GRPO基线和推理时模拟方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI代理强化学习方法虽然在移动平台上展现出强大的环境学习能力，但RL训练通常需要大量的真实环境交互，导致高昂的资源成本和训练不稳定性。在GUI场景中这一问题尤为突出，因此需要一种无需真实环境交互即可高效训练的方法。

Method: WM-R1包含三个关键设计：(1) 用世界模型替代真实Android环境作为所有rollout的状态转移来源；(2) 将世界模型嵌入代理的思维过程，使代理在做出最终动作前能推理候选动作的后果；(3) 引入多维规则奖励，联合优化任务成功率、轨迹效率和世界模型利用率。此外，精心构建了包含2000个挑战性任务的高质量数据集用于高效训练，并支持基于世界模型的级联并行化及步骤级细粒度轨迹生成。

Result: 在Android移动基准上的实验表明，WM-R1训练的代理显著优于仅使用GRPO的基线和推理时模拟方法。

Conclusion: WM-R1证明了用世界模型替代真实环境进行GUI代理RL训练的可行性，消除了对真实环境交互的依赖，同时通过并行化、细粒度轨迹生成和多维奖励机制实现了更高效和更稳定的训练，为移动GUI代理的RL训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: GUI agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) have showcased strong environment learning capabilities on mobile platforms. However, RL typically demands extensive real-environment interactions, leading to high resource costs and instability, especially in GUI scenarios. To address these, we propose WM-R1, the first reinforcement learning framework that trains mobile GUI agents with world models instead of real environments. Specifically, world models serve as the source of state transitions during all rollouts, replacing the real Android environment within the training loop. WM-R1 also embeds world models directly into the thinking process, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of candidate actions before committing to the final action. Crucially, WM-R1 eliminates the need for real-environment interaction, supports massively parallelized and step-level granularized trajectory generation grounded in world models, and introduces a multi-dimensional rule-based reward that jointly optimizes task success, trajectory efficiency, and world model utilization. For efficient training, we curate a high-quality dataset of 2000 challenging tasks. Experiments on Android mobile benchmarks demonstrate that WM-R1-trained agents significantly outperform GRPO-only baselines and inference-time simulation methods. Code is available at https://github.com/genalyu/WM-R1 .

</details>


### [112] [SETU: An Agentic Ecosystem for Multilingual, Persona-Aware Communication Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27524)
*Jonnalagadda Maruthi Tejas,Uponika Barman Roy,Tilottama Goswami,Samir Goswami,Mousita Dhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SETU，一个面向企业沟通教练的智能体生态系统，通过分解视频、音频、文本、评分、通知和报告等专用智能体，经信任感知编排协调，生成模态归因的教练报告，帮助招聘者、前线销售和培训团队改善跨语言受众沟通。系统设计支持两个场景，但论文仅报告销售演讲场景的实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业培训团队需要可扩展且可解释的工具来改善多语言环境下的工作沟通。现有系统要么孤立地评分文本、音频或视频，要么产生难以审计的黑盒输出，不适合教练用途。

Method: 构建SETU智能体生态系统，将分析分解为专门的视频、音频语音、文本相关性、评分、通知和报告智能体，通过信任感知编排协调。系统生成模态归因的教练报告，供形成性培训使用，人类审核者保留最终判断权。针对招聘者-候选人通话和销售演讲两个场景设计，论文仅评估销售演讲场景。

Result: 由于评估资源有限，论文仅报告场景（ii）即目标受众适配的销售演讲场景的结果。

Conclusion: SETU通过信任感知的智能体编排和模态级归因报告，弥合跨区域语言与受众期望之间的沟通差距，同时保留人类审核者的最终判断权，为形成性培训提供可审计的教练支持。

Abstract: Corporate training teams need scalable and explainable tools to improve workforce communication in multilingual settings. Existing systems often score text, audio, or video in isolation, or produce black-box outputs that are difficult to audit for coaching use. This paper presents SETU, an agentic ecosystem for corporate communication coaching aimed at recruiters, frontline sales professionals and training units who prepare for audience specific conversations. SETU is designed for two scoped scenarios: (i) recruiter-candidate eligibility-and-interest calls with persona context and (ii) sales pitches with target-audience adaptation; owing to limited evaluation resources, this paper reports results on scenario (ii) only. The ecosystem decomposes analysis into specialized video, audio-speech, text-relevance, scoring, notification and reporting agents coordinated through trust-aware orchestration. It generates modality-attributed coaching reports for formative training, with human reviewers retaining final judgment. The name SETU (bridge in several Indic languages) reflects the goal of bridging communication gaps across regional languages and audience expectations.

</details>


### [113] [Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator: A Compact Multimodal, Multilingual, and Reasoning Enabled Content Safety Moderator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27548)
*Varun Singh,Anuj Doshi,Makesh Narsimhan Sreedhar,Shaona Ghosh,Katherine Luna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator（Nemotron 3.5 CS），一个紧凑的4B视觉语言安全审核模型，可同时分类用户提示、图像和助手响应，覆盖12种语言，支持延迟敏感的安全标签输出和基于自定义策略的推理痕迹生成，并配套发布多模态多语言安全训练数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI应用的安全审核系统正从纯文本扩展到图像、文档、截图等模态，且需适配不同领域的自定义策略。现有防护措施通常只覆盖部分场景，难以同时兼顾广泛覆盖、自定义策略控制和低计算成本，亟需一个综合解决方案。

Method: Nemotron 3.5 CS是一个4B参数的视觉语言安全审核模型，联合分类用户提示、图像和助手响应，支持12种语言。它提供两档输出：延迟敏感场景下直接返回安全标签，需要推理时生成简洁的推理轨迹并应用自定义策略识别违规类别。同时发布涵盖人工标注真实图像审核、良性视觉语言与文档任务、合成稀有风险与越狱案例、自定义策略示例的多模态多语言安全训练数据集。

Result: 在涵盖多模态安全、文本审核、多语言鲁棒性、自定义策略遵循、良性误报和延迟的全面评估中，Nemotron 3.5 CS展现了实用的覆盖权衡：在增加图像条件和策略条件审核能力的同时，与专门的安全审核模型保持广泛竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑的视觉语言审核模型可作为可部署的前线安全组件，推理功能可按需用于审计和策略审查场景，形成实用性强的分层安全方案。

Abstract: Safety moderation for deployed AI applications is moving beyond text-only prompts: systems increasingly need to judge images, documents, screenshots, and generated responses under policies that vary across domains. Existing guardrails usually cover only part of this setting, making it difficult to combine broad coverage, custom policy control, and low compute cost. We present Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator, also referred to as Nemotron 3.5 CS in this paper for brevity, a compact 4B vision-language safety moderator that jointly classifies user prompts, images, and assistant responses across 12 languages. Nemotron 3.5 CS returns safety labels for latency-sensitive moderation and can additionally produce concise reasoning traces that apply supplied custom policies and identify violated categories when reasoning is requested. We also release a multimodal and multilingual safety dataset for guard training, spanning human-labeled real-image moderation, benign vision-language and document tasks, synthetic rare-risk and jailbreak cases, and custom-policy examples. Across evaluations spanning multimodal safety, text moderation, multilingual robustness, custom-policy following, benign false positives, and latency, Nemotron 3.5 CS demonstrates a practical coverage tradeoff: it adds image-conditioned and policy-conditioned moderation while remaining broadly competitive with specialized guard models. These results suggest that compact vision-language moderators can serve as deployable front-line safety components, with reasoning used selectively for audit and policy review.

</details>


### [114] [LongGuard: Mechanistic Analysis and Training-Free Mitigation of Long-Context Failure in Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27580)
*Ziyang Chen,Xing Wu,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongGuard框架，系统评估、机制分析并缓解大语言模型安全护栏在长上下文场景下的失效问题。通过SafetyNIAH任务在0.25k-32k长度网格上测试15种主流护栏，发现不安全召回率平均下降超50%；通过三层次（注意力→logit→行为）机制分析定位失效原因为不安全"针"被成比例稀释；并基于此提出两种免训练缓解方法（Chunked Detection和Attention-Head Sharpening）及部署协议（Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing），在五个基准上分别提升六种护栏平均性能22%和13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全护栏（guardrails）几乎仅在短文本上训练和评估，导致其在长上下文下可能失效，但目前缺少对这一问题的系统性量化和机制层面的理解。作者希望回答三个问题：长上下文护栏失效有多严重？失效的机制是什么？能否在不重新训练的前提下缓解该问题？

Method: 1) 提出SafetyNIAH任务，在0.25k-32k长度网格上评估15种主流护栏，并使用Paired Benign-Fill vs. Needle-Repeat实验设计区分"绝对长度"与"不安全针成比例稀释"两种假设；2) 采用三层注意力-logit-行为联动分析定位失效机制，并识别出护栏特有的稀疏检索头；3) 提出两种免训练缓解方法：Chunked Detection（CD，分块检测）和Attention-Head Sharpening（AHS，注意力头锐化），以及Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing（CAHR）部署协议。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 随长度增加，15种护栏的平均不安全召回率单调下降超50%；(2) 失效归因于不安全针的成比例稀释而非绝对长度；(3) 注意力稀释→logit间隔压缩→检测决策崩溃的因果链在剔除长度因素后依然成立；(4) 识别出一组对护栏特化的稀疏检索头；(5) 在五个基准上，CAHR-CD和CAHR-AHS分别将六种护栏平均性能提升22%和13%。

Conclusion: 长上下文安全护栏失效是一个系统性且机制可解释的问题，其根因在于注意力对不安全信息的稀释而非上下文绝对长度本身。通过免训练的CD和AHS方法配合CAHR协议，可以有效恢复护栏在长上下文下的安全检测性能，为实际部署提供即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety guardrails serve as the last line of defense against harmful inputs and outputs of large language models (LLMs), yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on short text. We present LongGuard, a framework that evaluates, mechanistically analyzes, and mitigates long-context guardrail failure. We formulate the task as Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack (SafetyNIAH) over a 0.25k-32k length grid; across 15 mainstream guardrails, unsafe recall drops monotonically by more than 50% on average, and a paired Benign-Fill vs. Needle-Repeat design attributes the failure to proportional dilution of the unsafe needle rather than to absolute length.
